	USE TESTING ;
	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS 
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS 
	SELECT * FROM PROJECTS 

	SELECT * 
	FROM DEPARTMENTS 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES 
	ON DEPARTMENTS.DEPARTMENTID = EMPLOYEES.DEPARTMENTID

	SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'

		SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE DepartmentCode LIKE '%IT%' AND Salary > 75000 AND HireDate >= '2018-03-13' AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' 

		SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE DepartmentCode LIKE '%IT%' AND Salary > 50000 AND HireDate >= '2018-03-13' AND Status = 'active'


SELECT 
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYESS,
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Bonus,
		E.Email,
		E.Salary + E.BONUS AS TOTAL_SALARY_EMPLOYES,
		E.Salary + 10000 AS TOTAL_1000_INCRESED,
		E.Salary - 10000 AS TOTAL_1000_DECRESED,
		E.SALARY * 2 AS TOTAL_2_MULPTPLIES,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.Salary > 200000 THEN 'HIGH_SALARY' 
					ELSE 'LOW_SALARY'
			END AS TOTAL_SALARY_DETAILS,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.Bonus BETWEEN 15000 AND 50000 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE 'BONUS_IS_LESS'
			END AS BONUS_LEVEL,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.HIREDATE <= '2015-01-01' THEN 'VERY_OLD'
					WHEN E.HIREDATE BETWEEN '2015-01-01' AND '2020-01-01' THEN '5_YEARS_BEFORE'
					WHEN E.HIREDATE > '2020-01-01' THEN 'NEW_JOINING'
			END AS DATE_CHECKING,
		SUM(E.SALARY)  AS NEW_SALARY,
		MIN(E.SALARY)  AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(E.SALARY)  AS MAX_SALARY,
		AVG(E.SALARY)  AS AVG_SALARY
FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
WHERE GENDER = 'M' AND BONUS IS NOT NULL 
GROUP BY 
		D.DepartmentName, 
		D.DepartmentCode, 
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Bonus,
		E.Email
HAVING SUM(E.SALARY) > 150000 
ORDER BY E.SALARY DESC 


	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS D  
		INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID 
		INNER JOIN PROJECTS P
	ON D.DepartmentID = P.DepartmentID
		INNER JOIN EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS EP
	ON EP.ProjectID = P.ProjectID
		AND EP.ROLENAME = 'LEAD'


SELECT * , 
	E.SALARY + 1000 AS NEW_SALARY ,
	E.Salary - 1000 AS NEW_MINUS_SALARY,
	E.SALARY * 2 AS MULTIPLE_SALARY,
	E.SALARY / 10 AS DIVIDED_SALARY,
	E.Salary + E.Bonus AS SALARY_Bonus,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.DATEOFBIRTH BETWEEN '1990-01-01' AND '1995-01-01' THEN '35_AGE'
				ELSE 'BELOW_30'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.HireDate < '2015-01-01' THEN 'OLD_EMPLOYESS'
				WHEN E.HireDate BETWEEN '2015-01-01' AND '2020-01-01' THEN 'MIDDLE_EMPLOYEE'
				WHEN E.HireDate  > '2020-01-01' THEN 'NEW_EMPLOYEES'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.SALARY < 50000 THEN 'SALARY IS LOWER'
				WHEN E.Salary BETWEEN  50000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL_SALARY'
				WHEN E.Salary > 150000 THEN 'HIGH_SALARY'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.ExperienceYears < 3 THEN 'LOW_EXPERIENCE'
				WHEN E.ExperienceYears BETWEEN  3 AND 8 THEN 'HIGH'
				WHEN E.Salary > 8 THEN 'NOT_POSSIBLE'
		END

	FROM DEPARTMENTS  D  
			LEFT JOIN EMPLOYEES E
			ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
			LEFT JOIN PROJECTS P
			ON D.DepartmentID = P.DepartmentID
			LEFT JOIN EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS EP
			ON P.ProjectID = EP.ProjectID
	WHERE E.Gender = 'M'
	ORDER BY D.DepartmentID DESC 


	SELECT COUNT (*) , DepartmentName FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	GROUP BY DepartmentName
	HAVING COUNT (*)  = 3 
	ORDER BY DepartmentName DESC 

	
	SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode, 
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location , 
		CAST(D.CreatedDate AS DATE) AS DATE_FORMAT,
		CONCAT(E.FIRSTNAME , E.LASTNAME) AS TOTAL_NAME ,
				CASE 
					WHEN E.Gender = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
					WHEN E.Gender = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		YEAR(E.DATEOFBIRTH) AS YEAR_ONLY,
		YEAR(E.HireDate) AS YEAR_ONLY_HIREDATE,
				CASE 
					WHEN ManagerID IS NULL THEN '0'
					ELSE ManagerID
				END AS UPDATE_MANAGER_ID , 

				CASE 
					WHEN BONUS IS NULL THEN '0'
					ELSE BONUS
				END AS BONUS_VALUE_UPDATED ,

				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 50000 AND 150000  THEN 'MEDIMUM'
					WHEN SALARY > 150000 THEN 'HIGH'
				END AS UPDATED_SALARY,
			   SUM(E.SALARY) OVER (
								PARTITION BY E.DepartmentID
							) AS TOTAL_SALARY
		FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
			RIGHT JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
			ON D. DepartmentID = E. DepartmentID 
		WHERE Gender  = 'M' AND LOCATION = 'MUMBAI'
	

	SELECT COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_COUNT, DepartmentName  FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	FULL OUTER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'
	GROUP BY DepartmentName
	HAVING COUNT(*) = 2

	-----------------------------------------------------------CROSS JOIN IT IS USED LESS IN DATA ENGINEERING --------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS D
	CROSS JOIN EMPLOYEES E
	-----------------------------------------------------------SELF JOIN DATA ENGINEERING ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
	---Find Employees Who Earn More Than Their Manager -------------------
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES

	SELECT * FROM PROJECTS

	SELECT 
	E.FIRSTNAME AS EMPLOYEENAME ,
	E.SALARY AS EMPLYEE_SALARY,
	M.FIRSTNAME AS MANAGERNAME ,
	M.Salary AS MANAGER_SALARY
	FROM EMPLOYEES E 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES M 
	ON E.MANAGERID = M.EMPLOYEEID 
	WHERE E.Salary > M.Salary AND E.Status = 'ACTIVE' 
	ORDER BY M.FirstName
	

	SELECT 
	M.FIRSTNAME AS MANAGER_NAME,
	COUNT(E.EmployeeID) AS TOTAL_MANAGER_COUNT
	FROM EMPLOYEES E 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES M
	ON E.ManagerID = M.EmployeeID
	GROUP BY M.FirstName
	HAVING COUNT(E.EmployeeID) > 2;

	UNION

WITH FINAL_DATA AS
(

	SELECT 
			UPPER(EMPLOYEENAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(DEPARTMENT) AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
				CASE
					WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'SALARY_IS_LOW' 
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE'HIGH'
				END AS NEW_DATA
		FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
UNION
		SELECT 
			UPPER(EMPLOYEENAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(DEPARTMENT) AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
				CASE
					WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'SALARY_IS_LOW' 
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 150001 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE'HIGH'
				END AS NEW_DATA
		FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
) 

	SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
	WHERE NEW_DATA = 'NORMAL';

---------------------------------------------UNION WITH AGGRICATION + CTE----------------

WITH DATA_CHK AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

		UNION 

		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
	),

	FINAL_OUTPUT AS 

	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENT,
			COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES,
			SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
			MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
			FROM DATA_CHK
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
			HAVING COUNT(*) = 2
	),

	FINAL_OUTPUT_VEERA AS

	(
		SELECT * FROM FINAL_OUTPUT 
		WHERE TOTAL_SALARY < 250000
	)

		SELECT * FROM FINAL_OUTPUT_VEERA
		WHERE Department = 'HR'

	WITH DATA_CHK AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

		UNION ALL

		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
	)

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENT,
			COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES,
			SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
			MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
			FROM DATA_CHK
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
			HAVING COUNT(*) >= 2
	
-------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE UNION ALL -------------------------------

WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM SELECTED_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM SOUTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
	),
SECOND_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA
		WHERE Department IN ('IT' ,'SALES' , 'SUPPORT')
	),
THIRD_DATA AS 
	(

		SELECT * FROM SECOND_DATA
		WHERE EmployeeID >= 105
	),
FINAL_DATA AS 
	(	
		SELECT * , 
			CASE
				WHEN Salary < 100000 THEN 'LOW'
				ELSE 'HIGH'
			END AS SALARY_CATEGORY
		FROM THIRD_DATA
	),


FINAL_DATA_FINAL AS
		
	(	
		SELECT 
		Department,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM FINAL_DATA
		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT 
	)
		
		SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA_FINAL
		WHERE DEPARTMENT = 'IT'
		
----------------------------------SET OPERATOR INTERSECT -------------------------------------------
WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		INTERSECT
		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

	)		
		SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA
		WHERE Department = 'IT'


		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		EXCEPT
		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		EXCEPT
		SELECT * FROM SELECTED_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		EXCEPT
		SELECT * FROM SOUTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

---------------------------------------------------------------------- SUB QUERY WITH FROM LOCATION -----------------------------------------------------
		
	SELECT * FROM 
		(	
			SELECT 
				CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
				GENDER,
				EMAIL,
				STATUS,
				PHONE,
				SALARY ,
					CASE 
						WHEN Salary < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
						WHEN Salary BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
						WHEN Salary BETWEEN 150000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
						ELSE 'HIGH'
					END AS SALARY_TYPE
			FROM EMPLOYEES
		) AS DT
	WHERE SALARY_TYPE = 'HIGH'

	
	SELECT * FROM 
			
		(
			SELECT 
				DepartmentID,
				COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYESS,
				SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_EMP_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES
			GROUP BY DepartmentID
		) AS T
	WHERE T.TOTAL_EMP_SALARY BETWEEN 200000 AND 300000
	ORDER BY T.TOTAL_EMP_SALARY DESC 
	
	WITH FINAL_DATA	AS 	
			(	
				SELECT 
					CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
					GENDER,
					EMAIL,
					STATUS,
					PHONE,
					SALARY ,
						CASE 
							WHEN Salary < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
							WHEN Salary BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
							WHEN Salary BETWEEN 150000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
							ELSE 'HIGH'
						END AS SALARY_TYPE
				FROM EMPLOYEES
			)
	SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
	WHERE SALARY_TYPE = 'LOW'

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------sub query with select location -----------------------------------------------
	USE TESTING 

	SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA ORDER BY SALARY DESC 
	
	SELECT 
		DEPARTMENT,
		CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		UPPER(DEPARTMENT) AS NEW_DEPARTMENT,
		UPPER(CITY) AS NEW_CITY,
		UPPER(STATE) AS NEW_STATE,
		UPPER(COUNTRY) AS NEW_COUNTRY,
			(SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA)
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE SALARY > 150000
--------------------------------------------------------------GROUP BU SUBQUERY ---------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
		    DEPARTMENT,
			(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM EMP_DATA AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE ),
			(SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA),
			(SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA)
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE SALARY > 150000
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENT ;

---------------------------------------------------CONDITIONAL SUBQUERY ---------------------------------------------------------
WITH FINAL_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT 
				DEPARTMENT,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA)
					THEN 'GOOD'
					ELSE 'BAD'
					END AS NEW_DATA,
				CASE 
					WHEN BONUS >
						(SELECT AVG(BONUS) FROM EMP_DATA)
					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE 'NOT_OK'
					END AS NEW_DATA_1	
		FROM EMP_DATA
	)
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
WHERE NEW_DATA = 'GOOD' AND NEW_DATA_1 = 'OK'

	SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , ES.FIRSTNAME , ES.LASTNAME) ,
			CASE
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			END AS NEW_GENDER,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.LOCATION
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES
	WHERE SALARY > 
		(	
			SELECT 
				AVG(ES2.SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
			WHERE ES.DEPARTMENTID = ES2.DEPARTMENTID
		)


	SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , ES.FIRSTNAME , ES.LASTNAME) ,
			CASE
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			END AS NEW_GENDER,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.LOCATION
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES
	WHERE SALARY <
		(	
			SELECT 
				SUM(ES2.BONUS) AS TOTAL_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
			WHERE ES.DEPARTMENTID = ES2.DEPARTMENTID
		)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------MULTI ROW SUB QUERY --------------------------------------------

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			AGE
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE DepartmentID IN  --------------- IN USED FOR MULTIPLE ROWS DATA ----------------IN MENAS ONLY PARTICULAR CITY---------------------------
	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
	)
	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			AGE
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE DepartmentID NOT IN  --------------- NOT IN USED FOR MULTIPLE ROWS DATA ----------------------- NOT IN USED FOR EXCPT THAT CITY --------------
	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
	)

		SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY > ANY ------------------------------------------ANY IS USED Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery--------------
	(
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)

	SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY < SOME ------------------------------------------SOME  IS USED Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery--------------
	(	
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)
	---------------------BOTH SOME AND ANY IS SAME LOGIC ONLY /////Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery===================
	SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY < ALL -------------------------Condition must be true for every value returned by the subquery-----------------------------
	(	
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)

---------------=====================================================FROM SUBQUERY==========================================--------------------------

	SELECT *
		FROM 
			(
				SELECT
				UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
				CASE 
						WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
						WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
				END AS NEW_GENDER,
				YEAR(HIREDATE)AS NEW_YEAR
				FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
			)AS S
	WHERE NEW_GENDER = 'FEMALE'
		
	
	SELECT * FROM 
			(
				SELECT 
					UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
					CASE 
						WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'NOTOK'
						WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
						ELSE 'HIGH'
					END AS NEW_SALARY,
					CASE 
						WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
						WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
					END AS NEW_GENDER,
					YEAR (HIREDATE) AS NEW_YEAR,
					SUBSTRING(EMAIL , 0 , 10) AS NEW_MAIL_ID
					FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
			) AS NEW
	WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'OK'

	---------------=====================================================SELECT  SUBQUERY==========================================--------------------------		
	SELECT 
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS  NEW_LAST_NAME,
		SALARY,
		LOCATION,
				(SELECT SUM(SALARY) AS SUM_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT MAX(SALARY) AS  MAX_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT COUNT(*)  AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY )
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI';

------------------------------------------------------------------- 25-08-2026---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		CASE
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER,
		CASE
			WHEN DEPARTMENTID < 20 THEN 'OLD'
			WHEN DEPARTMENTID BETWEEN 20 AND 40 THEN 'NORMAL'
			ELSE 'NEW'
		END AS NEW_DEPARTMENTID,
		(
			SELECT AVG(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS AVG_SALARY,

		(
			SELECT SUM(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS SUM_SALARY,
		(
			SELECT MIN(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS MIN_SALARY,

		(
			SELECT MAX(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS MAX_SALARY , 

		SALARY - (SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY) AS DETECTION_SALARY , 

		CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY) THEN 'OK'
				ELSE'NOTOK' 
		END NEW_SALARY_DETAILS,
		DATEDIFF (YEAR , HireDate , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_YEAR
  
FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY;


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------NSESTED SUBQUERY LOGICS ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------Find employees who work in departments located in Chennai.-----------------------------------
 
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN 
		(SELECT DEPARTMENTID  FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI')

--------------------Find employees whose salary is greater than the average salary of employees working in Chennai departments.---------------------------------------

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT AVG(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)
	
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT MIN(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT MAX(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary < 
		(
			SELECT SUM(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)
--------------------------Find employees working in departments that have employees, where those departments are located in Chennai.------------------------------------------------
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN 
		(	
			SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID HAVING  COUNT(*) >= 1 AND DEPARTMENTID IN 
				(SELECT DEPARTMENTID  FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI')
		)

	
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT AVG(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(
					SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE') AND DEPARTMENTID IN 
						(
							SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE Status = 'COMPLETED' AND ProjectID IN 
								(
									SELECT ProjectID FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE Role IN ('DATA ENGINEER' , 'ANALYST' , 'MANAGER' , 'TESTER' , 'MANAGER') AND ProjectID IN 
										(
											SELECT ProjectID FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE StartDate BETWEEN '2024-01-01' AND '2025-01-01'
										)
								)
						)
				)
		);


 (
	SELECT * FROM 	
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			SALARY,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)

					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE'NOTOK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	) T
	WHERE NEW_LOGIC = 'NOTOK' ) 

	
	SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			SALARY,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)
					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE'NOTOK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC,
				CASE 
					WHEN Bonus > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(Bonus) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)
					THEN 'BONUS_OK'
					ELSE'BONUS_NOT_OK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	
-------------===================================================correlated Sub Query=======================================================--------------------------------------------------------------------

	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary > 
				(
					SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)


		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary < 
				(
					SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)


		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary > 
				(
					SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)

		
		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE
				(
					SELECT COUNT(*) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				) > 1

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------	Correlated Subqueries WITH CASE -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY,
			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'ABOVE_DEPARTMENT_SALARY' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'EQUAL' 
				ELSE 'NOT_EQUAL'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC,

			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT MAX(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'OK' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT MAX(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'TK' 
				ELSE 'PK'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC,

			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT MIN(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'MIN_OK' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT MIN(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'MIN_NOT' 
				ELSE 'HIGH'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC	
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------Multiple Correlated Subqueries-----------------------------------------------------------------------			
	SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE,
			(
				SELECT  AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_AVG_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_SUM_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MIN_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MAX_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT COUNT(*) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MAX_SALARY 

	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
	
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------Multiple Correlated Subqueries + CASE------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 SELECT * FROM 

 (SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE,
			(
				SELECT  AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_AVG_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_SUM_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MIN_SALARY ,

		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'OK'
			ELSE 'NOT_OK'
		END AS AVG_NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'VEERAMANI'
			WHEN SALARY = 
				(
					SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'EUQAL_AVERAGE'
			ELSE 'NOT_QUAL'
		END AS NEW_LOGICS,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'MIN_SALARY'
			WHEN SALARY = 
				(
					SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'CHECKINH'
			ELSE 'SALARY_OK'
		END AS MIN_SALARY_CHECKING
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES ) T
	WHERE T.AVG_NEW_SALARY = 'OK'
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------EXISTS   Beginner Level--------------------------------------------
	
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.GENDER,
		ES.SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	    WHERE EXISTS          ----------------------------------------EXISTS checks whether at least one matching row exists in the subquery.----------------
	(
		SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
		WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
	)

	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.GENDER,
		ES.SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	    WHERE EXISTS 
	(
		SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
		WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
		AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M' ----------------------------------------WHERE + Multiple Conditions---------------------------------------
	)
---------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE EXISTS CONDITION WITH AND -------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.GENDER,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	WHERE EXISTS  -------------------------FIRST EXIT CONFITION -----------------------------------------------
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS 
			WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID 
			AND DS.LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
		)
	 AND EXISTS  ---------------------------------------------SECOND EXITS CONDITION ---------------------------------------
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE ES.EMPLOYEEID = EPS.EMPLOYEEID
			
		)

----------------------------------------------MULTIPLE EXSITS CONDITION WITH AND CHECKING PURPOSE ------------------------------------------------------
SELECT * FROM 
	(
		SELECT 
			UPPER(E.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
			UPPER(E.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LASTNAME,
			E.SALARY,
			E.AGE AS NEW_AGE
		    FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS E
			WHERE E.SALARY > 100000
		AND EXISTS 
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS D
				WHERE D.DEPARTMENTID = E.DEPARTMENTID 
				AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' 
			)
		AND EXISTS 
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS P
				WHERE P.EMPLOYEEID = E.EMPLOYEEID
			)

		)tS 
WHERE tS.NEW_AGE > 35
----------------------------------------------------NON EXSITS CONDITION CHECKING ------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES 
	WHERE NOT EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE EPS.EMPLOYEEID = ES.EMPLOYEEID
		)

	
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES 
	WHERE  EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
			WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
		)
	AND NOT EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE EPS.EMPLOYEEID = ES.EMPLOYEEID
		)


----------------------------------------------CTE ---- COMMON TABLE EXPRESSION ----------------------------------------------
----- WHAT IS CTE --- IT IS COMMON TABLE EXPRESSION ------
----A CTE is a named temporary result set that you define using WITH and then use in the query immediately following it.------------------------
 
 WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
 (
	SELECT	
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS LAST_NAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS FULL_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY < 70000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 70000  AND 100000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN BONUS < 20000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN BONUS BETWEEN 20000  AND 40000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_BONUS,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS ONLY_YEAR,
		LOCATION,
		AGE 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA  WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'MEDIUM'


-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------26-08-2026---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-----------------------------------------------------------CTE WITH WHERE CONDITION LOGICS--------------------------------------------------------------------------
 WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
 (
	SELECT	
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS LAST_NAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS FULL_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY < 70000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 70000  AND 100000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN BONUS < 20000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN BONUS BETWEEN 20000  AND 40000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_BONUS,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS ONLY_YEAR,
		LOCATION,
		AGE 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'
)
SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA  WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'MEDIUM';

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE WITH SELECTED COLUMNS --------------------------------------------------------------------------

WITH DATA_CHECKING AS 	
(	SELECT 
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		GENDER,
		DEPARTMENT,
		CITY,
		STATE,
		SALARY,
		HIREDATE,
		BONUS,
		AGE,
		STATUS
	FROM EMP_DATA
)
SELECT * FROM DATA_CHECKING ;

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE WITH SELECTED COLUMNS --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH CAL_COLUMN AS 
(
	SELECT 
		UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' ,FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF'), 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER_DIFFERENT_FORMAT,--------------------WIHTOUT CASE I USED CHOOSE AND CHAIRINDEX FOR CONDITION 
		IIF (GENDER = 'M', 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') NEW_IF_TYPE,-----------------------------------------------WITHOUT CASE I USED IIF CONDITION FOR THIS SECTIONS
		TRIM(DEPARTMENT) AS SAPCE_REMOVED_DEPARTMENT,
		TRIM(CITY) AS NEW_CITY,
		TRIM(STATE) AS NEW_STATE,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
		SALARY + 10000 AS NEW_INCREMENT_SALARY,
		SALARY - 10000 AS NEW_DECREMENT_SALARY,
		SALARY + BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_BONUS,
		SALARY / 10 AS DIVIDED_SALARY,
		SALARY * 2 AS MULTIPLE_SALARY,
		SALARY % 100 AS MODULE_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
)
SELECT * FROM CAL_COLUMN WHERE NEW_CITY = 'CHENNAI'	
(
	SELECT 
		DEPARTMENT,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
-----------------------------------------------------------CTE + GROUP BY CHECKING --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH GROUPBY_SUMMARY AS 
(
	SELECT 
		DEPARTMENT,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
)
SELECT * FROM GROUPBY_SUMMARY;

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE + GROUP BY CHECKING + HAVING CHECKING  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH HAVING_CHECKING AS 

		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
		HAVING MAX(SALARY) < 150000 AND DEPARTMENT IN ('IT' , 'HR' , 'QA')
)
SELECT * FROM HAVING_CHECKING;

-----------------------------------------------------------Multiple CTE's cHEKING   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
--------------------------THIS IS CALLLED AS CTE CHAINING -------------------------------------------
WITH 
	MULTIPLE_1 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEES 
			WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
		),

	MULTIPLE_2 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
			WHERE LOCATION  IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
			AND GENDER = 'M'
		),
	MULTIPLE_3 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMP_DATA 
			WHERE City  IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
			AND GENDER = 'M'
			AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
		),
	MULTIPLE_4 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY 
			WHERE ROLE  IN ('TESTER' , 'ANALYST')
		),
	MULTIPLE_5 AS 
		(
			SELECT 
				DEPARTMENT,
				COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
				SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
				AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
				MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
				MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
				FROM EMP_DATA
				GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
				HAVING MAX(SALARY) < 150000 AND DEPARTMENT IN ('IT' , 'HR' , 'QA')	
		),
	MULTIPLE_6 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM SOUTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
			WHERE Department  IN ('IT' , 'R&D')
		)
SELECT * FROM MULTIPLE_6 ;


--------------------------------------------------------CTE + JOIN-----------------------------------------
--------------------------------------------------------Very important for Data Engineering.-----------------------------------------
WITH JOIN_TESTING AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE' 
	)
					SELECT 
					UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , EP.FIRSTNAME , EP.LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
					CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(EP.GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
					YEAR(EP.DATEOFBIRTH) AS YEAR_ONLY,
					YEAR(EP.HIREDATE) AS YEAR_HIREDATE,
					EP.SALARY,
					EP.BONUS,
					EP.STATUS,
					DP.DEPARTMENTNAME,
					DP.LOCATION,
					YEAR(DP.CREATEDDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
					PS.PROJECTNAME,
					PS.BUDGET
					FROM JOIN_TESTING AS EP
						INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS AS DP
						ON EP.DEPARTMENTID = DP.DEPARTMENTID
						INNER JOIN PROJECTS AS PS
						ON PS.DEPARTMENTID = EP.DEPARTMENTID
					WHERE BONUS IS NOT NULL


------------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE CTE + JOIN------------------------------------------
WITH EMPLOYESS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS IN  ('ACTIVE' , 'INACTIVE')
	),
DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS 
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE' , 'MUMBAI' , 'DELHI')
	),
PROJECTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM PROJECTS 
		WHERE PROJECTSTATUS = 'ACTIVE'
	)
				
				SELECT 
					FIRSTNAME,
					LASTNAME,
					SALARY,
					GENDER
					FROM EMPLOYESS_DATA AS E 
					INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS D
					ON E.DEPARTMENTID = D.DEPARTMENTID
					INNER JOIN PROJECTS_DATA AS P
					ON E.DEPARTMENTID = P.DEPARTMENTID ;


------------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE CTE + JOIN + GROUP BY ------------------------------------------

WITH EMPLOYESS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS IN  ('ACTIVE' , 'INACTIVE')
	),
DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT *
			FROM DEPARTMENTS  
			
	)
		SELECT 
			DD.Location
			FROM EMPLOYESS_DATA AS ED
			INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS_DATA DD
			ON ED.DepartmentID = DD.DepartmentID
			WHERE GENDER = 'M'
			GROUP BY LOCATION ;

------------------------------------------------------------CTE + Subquery CHECKING ------------------------------------------

WITH SINGLE_SUBQUERY_CTE AS 
(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY > ------------------------SINGLE LINE SUBQUERY WITH CTE GREATER COMPARISON 
			(
				SELECT AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY  
			) 
		        AND  DEPARTMENTID IN 
			(
				SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
				WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
				AND MANAGERID IS NOT NULL 
			)

)
SELECT * FROM SINGLE_SUBQUERY_CTE;

WITH SINGLE_DATA AS 
(	  
	  SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT * FROM SINGLE_DATA 
	WHERE SALARY >  -----------SINGLE LINE SUB ----- AVG-----------------QUERY TESTING -------------------

(
	SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM SINGLE_DATA
)

-----------MULTI LINE SUB QUERY TESTING ------------------- IN SUB QUERY -----------------------------------------------

WITH MULTIPLE_TESTING_IN AS 
(	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT  * FROM MULTIPLE_TESTING_IN 
	WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN --------------------------MULTIPLE SUB QUERY CTE  , THIS QUERY IS JOINING THE ABOVE QUERY AND BELOW QUERY 

(	
	SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
	WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
)

-----------MULTI LINE SUB QUERY TESTING ------------------- NOT IN SUB QUERY -----------------------------------------------


WITH MULTIPLE_TESTING_NOT_IN AS 
(	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT  * FROM MULTIPLE_TESTING_NOT_IN 
	WHERE DEPARTMENTID NOT IN --------------------------MULTIPLE SUB QUERY CTE  , THIS QUERY IS JOINING THE ABOVE QUERY AND BELOW QUERY 

(	
	SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
	WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
)
----------------------------------------------- CTE WITH ANY QUERY CHECKING ----------------------------------------------
WITH ANY_CTE_CHEKING AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			EMPLOYEEID,
			SALARY,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
		SELECT * FROM ANY_CTE_CHEKING
		WHERE SALARY > ANY  -------------- MULTIPLE SUBQUERY WITH CTE AND ANY ------ ANY MEANS ALL THE RECORDS WILL COME ABOVE 1099 EMPLOYEE ID RECORDS
(
		SELECT SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1099'
)

			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
		SELECT * FROM ANY_CTE_CHEKING
		WHERE SALARY > SOME  -------------- MULTIPLE SUBQUERY WITH CTE AND SOME ------ 
(
		SELECT SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1099'
)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- CTE WITH ALL QUERY CHECKING ----------------------------------------------

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			EMPLOYEEID,
			SALARY,

			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,

			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY

		SELECT * FROM ALL_CTE_CHEKING
		WHERE SALARY > ALL  -------------- MULTIPLE SUBQUERY WITH CTE AND ALL ------ ANY 
(
		SELECT SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1011'
)

USE VEERAMANI 
SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA
SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA WHERE EmployeeID =2 
SELECT FIRSTNAME AS NEW_FIRSTNAME , GENDER AS NEW_FULL_NAME , DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT ,  STATE AS NEW_STATE FROM EMP_DATA 
SELECT DISTINCT EMPLOYEEID,  FIRSTNAME AS NEW_FIRSTNAME , GENDER AS NEW_FULL_NAME , DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT ,  STATE AS NEW_STATE FROM EMP_DATA 

SELECT DISTINCT EMPLOYEEID,
FIRSTNAME AS NEW_NAME, 
GENDER AS FULL_GENDER, 
DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
STATE AS NEW_STATE, 
AGE AS NEW_AGE, 
SALARY AS NEW_SALRY, 
PHONE AS NEW_PHONE_NUMBER, 
STATUS FROM EMP_DATA 
WHERE EMPLOYEEID BETWEEN 1 AND 100 
ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID  
OFFSET 90 ROWS 
FETCH NEXT 2 ROWS ONLY;


SELECT DISTINCT EMPLOYEEID , 
GENDER AS NEW_GENDER ,
DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT , 
CITY AS NEW_CITY , 
STATE AS NEW_STATE , 
HIREDATE AS NEW_HIREDATE , 
SALARY AS NEW_SALRY , 
BONUS AS NEW_BONUS , 
AGE AS NEW_AGE , 
PHONE AS NEW_PHONE , 
STATUS FROM EMP_DATA 
WHERE EMPLOYEEID BETWEEN 1 AND 50 
ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID  
OFFSET 10 ROWS 
FETCH NEXT 20 ROWS ONLY;


SELECT DISTINCT EMPLOYEEID,
GENDER AS NEW_GENDER, 
DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
CITY AS NEW_CITY, 
STATE AS NEW_STATE,
HIREDATE AS NEW_HIREDATE,
AGE AS NEW_AGE ,
SALARY AS OLD_SALARY,
SALARY + 10000 AS NEW_SALARY ,
SALARY - 10000 AS NEW_SALARY_MINUS,
SALARY * 2 AS NEW_SALARY_MULTIPLE ,
SALARY / 2 AS NEW_SALARY_DIVIDED,
SALARY % 1000 AS NEW_SALARY_MODULES
FROM EMP_DATA 
WHERE EmployeeID BETWEEN 1 AND 100 
ORDER BY  EmployeeID  
OFFSET 15 ROWS 
FETCH NEXT 15 ROWS ONLY 

SELECT DISTINCT EMPLOYEEID, 
GENDER AS NEW_GENDER, 
DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
HIREDATE AS NEW_HIREDATE, 
SALARY AS OLD_SALARY, 
BONUS AS OLD_BONUS ,
SALARY + BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_INCREMENT,
SALARY - BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_DECEREMENT,
SALARY * 2 AS NEW_BIG_SALARY,
SALARY /10 AS NEW_DIVIDED_SALARY,
SALARY % 1000 AS NEW_MODULES
FROM EMP_DATA 
WHERE 
EmployeeID BETWEEN 1 AND 200 AND  
SALARY + BONUS > 100000  AND GENDER ='M'
ORDER BY SALARY + BONUS  
OFFSET 51 ROWS 
FETCH NEXT 50 ROWS ONLY


SELECT DISTINCT
    EMPLOYEEID,
    GENDER AS NEW_GENDER,
    DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT,
    HIREDATE AS NEW_HIREDATE,
    SALARY AS OLD_SALARY,
    BONUS AS OLD_BONUS,
    SALARY + BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_INCREMENT,
    SALARY - BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_DECEREMENT,
    SALARY * 2 AS NEW_BIG_SALARY,
    SALARY / 10 AS NEW_DIVIDED_SALARY,
    SALARY % 1000 AS NEW_MODULES
FROM EMP_DATA
WHERE Department = 'HR'
  AND HireDate >= '2025-12-01'
  AND Salary >= 100000
  AND Bonus <= 20000
  AND Gender = 'M'
  AND HireDate <> '2025-12-14'
  AND HireDate != '2025-12-10'
ORDER BY Salary DESC;

SELECT DISTINCT
    EMPLOYEEID,
    GENDER AS NEW_GENDER,
    DEPARTMENT AS NEW_DEPARTMENT,
	CITY AS NEW_CITY,
    HIREDATE AS NEW_HIREDATE,
    SALARY AS OLD_SALARY,
    BONUS AS OLD_BONUS,
    SALARY + BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_INCREMENT,
    SALARY - BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_DECEREMENT,
    SALARY * 2 AS NEW_BIG_SALARY,
    SALARY / 10 AS NEW_DIVIDED_SALARY,
    SALARY % 1000 AS NEW_MODULES
FROM EMP_DATA
WHERE EMPLOYEEID BETWEEN 1 AND 200 AND Gender = 'F' AND DEPARTMENT = 'HR' AND Salary > 100000 AND  (CITY = 'CHENNAI' OR CITY = 'DELHI') AND NOT SALARY = 178608
ORDER BY HIREDATE DESC 

SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA WHERE EMPLOYEEID BETWEEN 1 AND 2000 AND (Department = 'QA' OR Department = 'HR') AND Status = 'ACTIVE'  AND NOT GENDER = 'F' AND NOT ISACTIVE = 1  AND (CITY = ' CHENNAI' OR CITY  = 'PUNE')

FROM EMP_DATA

  AND (Department = 'QA' OR Department = 'HR')
  AND Status = 'ACTIVE'
  AND Gender <> 'F'
  AND IsActive = 0
  AND (City = 'CHENNAI' OR City = 'PUNE');

	AND Department IN ('HR' , 'SUPPORT')

	AND City IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')

---------------------------------------------------------------------------AGGRECATE FUNCTION GROUP ---------------------------------------------------------------

	SELECT* FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT DEPARTMENT  FROM EMP_DATA GROUP BY DEPARTMENT ;
	SELECT CITY FROM EMP_DATA GROUP BY CITY
	SELECT GENDER FROM EMP_DATA GROUP BY GENDER 
	SELECT GENDER , DEPARTMENT , CITY FROM EMP_DATA GROUP BY DEPARTMENT , CITY , GENDER 



	SELECT 
	DEPARTMENT , 
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES 
	FROM EMP_DATA GROUP BY DEPARTMENT

	SELECT 
	DEPARTMENT ,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYESS , 
	SUM (SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY , 
	SUM (BONUS) AS TOTA_BONUS,
	AVG(SALARY) AS TOTA_AVG_SALARY,
	AVG(BONUS) AS TOTAL_AVG_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
	FROM EMP_DATA 
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
	HAVING COUNT(*) < 500
	ORDER BY DEPARTMENT DESC 
	
	SELECT 
	DEPARTMENT ,
	GENDER,
	CITY,
	STATE,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES ,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	SUM(BONUS) AS TOTAL_BONUS,
	AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
	AVG(BONUS) AS AVG_BONUS,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE SALARY > 100000
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENT , GENDER , CITY ,STATE
	HAVING COUNT(*) < 10 AND SUM(SALARY) >100000 AND AVG(BONUS)> 30000
	ORDER BY DEPARTMENT DESC 
--------------------------------------------------------------CASE STATEMENT LOGIC --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
	DEPARTMENT,
	GENDER,
	CITY,
	STATE,
		CASE DEPARTMENT 
		WHEN 'HR' THEN 'HUMAN_RESOURCE'
		WHEN 'QA' THEN 'QUALITY_ACCESS'
		WHEN 'IT' THEN 'INFORMATION_TECHNOLOGIES'
		ELSE DEPARTMENT
		END 
	FROM EMP_DATA
	
	SELECT  * ,
	CASE STATUS 
	WHEN 'ACTIVE' THEN 'OK'
	WHEN 'ON_LEAVE' THEN 'NOTOK'
	WHEN 'RESIGNED' THEN 'NULL'
	WHEN 'INACTIVE' THEN 'DELETED'
	ELSE STATUS
	END 
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE CITY = 'CHENNAI'

	
	SELECT 
	DEPARTMENT ,
	CASE DEPARTMENT 
	WHEN  'IT' THEN 'INFORMATION'
	WHEN  'QA' THEN 'QUALITY_ACESS'
	WHEN  'R&D' THEN 'RESERCH_AND_DEVELOPEMENT'
	ELSE DEPARTMENT
	END AS UPDATED_COLUMN,
	GENDER,
	CASE GENDER
	WHEN 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
	WHEN 'M' THEN 'MALE'
	END AS UPDATED_NAME,
	STATUS,
	CASE STATUS
	WHEN 'ACTIVE' THEN '0K'
	ELSE 'NOT OK'
	END AS UPDATED_STATUS,
	SALARY,
	SUM(SALARY) AS NEW_SALARY,
	AVG(SALARY) AS NEW_AVG_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE DEPARTMENT  IN ('IT' , 'R&D' , 'ADMIN' , 'SUPPORT' , 'PRODUCTION')
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENT , GENDER , STATUS , SALARY 
	HAVING SUM(SALARY) > 190000
	ORDER BY SALARY DESC 
	
	SELECT 
	* ,
	CASE
    WHEN Salary BETWEEN 180000 AND 190000 THEN 'GOOD_SALARY'
    WHEN Salary BETWEEN 170000 AND 180000 THEN 'NORMAL_SALARY'
    WHEN Salary BETWEEN 120000 AND 170000 THEN 'ACCEPTABLE'
    WHEN Salary < 120000 THEN 'BAD'
    ELSE 'UNKNOWN'
	END AS Salary_Expectation
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE DEPARTMENT = 'IT'  AND CITY = 'CHENNAI'

--------------------------------------------NESTED CASE STATEMENTS --------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT  * , 
 CASE 
	WHEN  DEPARTMENT = 'IT' THEN 
			CASE
				WHEN Salary BETWEEN 180000 AND 190000 THEN 'GOOD_SALARY'
				WHEN Salary BETWEEN 170000 AND 180000 THEN 'NORMAL_SALARY'
				WHEN Salary BETWEEN 120000 AND 170000 THEN 'ACCEPTABLE'
				WHEN Salary < 120000 THEN 'BAD'
			END
	WHEN DEPARTMENT = 'HR' THEN 
			CASE 
				WHEN BONUS > 50000 THEN 'BONUS_IS_OK'
				ELSE 'NOTOK'
			END
END 
FROM EMP_DATA


SELECT * , 
	CASE 
		WHEN DEPARTMENT = 'IT' THEN 
			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 190000 THEN 'GOOD_SALARY'
				WHEN SALARY > 180000 AND SALARY <190000 THEN 'NORMAL_SALARY'
				WHEN SALARY > 150000 AND SALARY <180000 THEN 'OK'
				ELSE 'NOT_OK'
			END 
		WHEN CITY = 'CHENNAI' THEN 
			CASE 
				WHEN STATUS = 'ACTIVE' THEN 'OK'
				ELSE 'NOTOK'
			END 
		WHEN  GENDER = 'M' THEN
			CASE 
				WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE' 
				ELSE 'FEMALE'
			END
	END
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE CITY = 'CHENNAI'



------------------------------------------------------------------------------STRING FUNCTIONS -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	

	SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA

	SELECT TOP(5) UPPER(FIRSTNAME) , UPPER(LASTNAME), UPPER(DEPARTMENT) , UPPER (CITY) , UPPER (STATE) , UPPER (COUNTRY) , HireDate , Salary , Bonus, ExperienceYears , Age , UPPER(EMAIL) , PHONE , IsActive , Status FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5) LOWER (FIRSTNAME) , LOWER(LASTNAME), LOWER(DEPARTMENT) , LOWER (CITY) , LOWER (STATE) , LOWER (COUNTRY) , HireDate , Salary , Bonus, ExperienceYears , Age , LOWER(EMAIL) , PHONE , IsActive , Status FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5) LEN (FIRSTNAME) , LEN(LASTNAME), LEN(DEPARTMENT) , LEN (CITY) , LEN (STATE) , LEN (COUNTRY) , HireDate , Salary , Bonus, ExperienceYears , Age , LEN(EMAIL) , PHONE , IsActive , Status FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5) SUBSTRING(FIRSTNAME , 1, 3) , SUBSTRING (LASTNAME , 1 , 4) , GENDER , DEPARTMENT, CITY , STATE , COUNTRY, SUBSTRING(EMAIL , 1 , 4) , SUBSTRING (STATUS , 1, 2)  FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5)  LEFT(EMPLOYEECODE , 3) , LEFT(CITY , 3) , LEFT(EMAIL , 4) , LEFT(HIREDATE , 4) ,LEFT (PHONE , 5)  FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5)  RIGHT(EMPLOYEECODE , 3) , RIGHT(CITY , 3) , RIGHT(EMAIL , 4) , RIGHT(HIREDATE , 4) , RIGHT (PHONE , 5)  FROM EMP_DATA
	SELECT TOP(5) REPLACE(HIREDATE , '-' , '/' ) , REPLACE(SALARY , '.00' , '') , REPLACE (STATUS , '_' , '-----') FROM EMP_DATA 
	SELECT * , CONCAT(CITY , ' ' , STATE , ' ' , COUNTRY ) AS TOTAL_DETAILSS_SINGLE_COLUMN FROM EMP_DATA 
	SELECT *, CONCAT_WS ('-' , CITY , STATE , COUNTRY) AS WITH_SEPERATOR FROM EMP_DATA

	SELECT 
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) , 
		UPPER(LASTNAME) , 
		GENDER , 
		DEPARTMENT,
		UPPER(DEPARTMENT) , 
		LOWER(STATUS) , 
		LEN(EMPLOYEECODE) , 
		LEN(EMAIL) ,
		LEN(PHONE) ,
		SUBSTRING (FirstName , 1 , 3 ) 	  ,
		SUBSTRING(LastName , 1, 5)  , 
		LEFT(EMAIL , 4),
		RIGHT(PHONE , 5),
		REPLACE(HIREDATE , '-' , '/'),
		REPLACE(SALARY , '.00' , ''),
		CONCAT(CITY , ' ' , STATE , ' ', Country) ,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , CITY , STATE , COUNTRY) , 
		TRIM(STATE) , TRIM(COUNTRY) , TRIM(EMAIL) , LTRIM(STATE) , LTRIM(CITY), LTRIM(COUNTRY) , RTRIM(STATE) , RTRIM(CITY) , RTRIM(COUNTRY) ,
		CHARINDEX('N' , CITY) , CHARINDEX ('N' , State) , CHARINDEX ('@' , Email),
		REVERSE( EMPLOYEECODE) , REVERSE(HIREDATE) , REVERSE (PHONE),
		STUFF(EMPLOYEECODE , 4, 10 , '') , STUFF(COUNTRY , 4, 10 , ''),
		PATINDEX('%[P]%' , EMPLOYEECODE) , PATINDEX('%[M]%' , CITY) , PATINDEX ('%[1-2]%' , Email) 
		FROM EMP_DATA 
		
	
		SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA

		SELECT * , CHARINDEX('N' , CITY)   ,CHARINDEX ('N' , State) , CHARINDEX ('@' , Email) FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , REVERSE( EMPLOYEECODE) , REVERSE(HIREDATE) , REVERSE (PHONE)  FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT  STRING_AGG(CAST(DEPARTMENT AS varchar(MAX)) , '-') FROM EMP_DATA ---------------------------------THIS IS LITTLE BIT OF CONFUSION 
		SELECT  STRING_AGG(CAST(GENDER AS varchar(MAX)) , '_') FROM EMP_DATA ---------------------------THIS ALSO STRING_AGG FUNCTION 
		SELECT * , STUFF(EMPLOYEECODE , 4, 10 , '') , STUFF(COUNTRY , 4, 10 , '') FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , PATINDEX('%[P]%' , EMPLOYEECODE) ,PATINDEX('%[M]%' , CITY) ,PATINDEX ('%[1-2]%' , Email)  FROM EMP_DATA

------------------------------------------- DATE AND TIME FUNCTIONS ---------------------------------------------------------


		USE TESTING 
		SELECT * FROM EMP_DATA  

		SELECT * , GETDATE() FROM EMP_DATA 
		SELECT * , GETDATE() AS NEW_TODAY_DATE FROM EMP_DATA 
		SELECT CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS NEW_DATE FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , YEAR(GETDATE()) , YEAR (HIREDATE) AS NEW_DATE FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , MONTH(GETDATE()) , MONTH (HIREDATE) AS NEW_DATE FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , DAY(GETDATE()) , DAY (HIREDATE) AS NEW_DATE FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT * , 
			YEAR (GETDATE()), 
			MONTH(GETDATE()) , 
			DAY(GETDATE()) FROM EMP_DATA
		SELECT 
			EMPLOYEECODE,
			DEPARTMENT,
			CITY,
			HIREDATE,
			GETDATE() AS NEW_DATE,
			YEAR(GETDATE()) AS TODAY_YEAR,
			MONTH(GETDATE()) AS THIS_YEAR_MONTH,
			DAY(GETDATE()) AS THIS_YEAR_DATE
			FROM EMP_DATA
			WHERE CAST(HIREDATE AS DATE) < GETDATE() 

		SELECT 
			HIREDATE,
			GETDATE() AS NEW_DATE,
			YEAR(GETDATE()) AS TODAY_YEAR,
			MONTH(GETDATE()) AS THIS_YEAR_MONTH,
			DAY(GETDATE()) AS THIS_YEAR_DATE,
			CAST(DATEADD(DAY , 1 , GETDATE()) AS DATE) AS DATE_ADD_1,
			CAST(DATEADD(MONTH , 1 , GETDATE()) AS DATE) AS DATE_MONTH_1,
			CAST(DATEADD(YEAR , 1 , GETDATE()) AS DATE) AS DATE_EAR_1,
			CAST(DATEADD(DAY , 1 , HIREDATE) AS DATE) AS DATE_ADD_2,
			CAST(DATEADD(MONTH , 1 , HIREDATE) AS DATE) AS DATE_MONTH_2,
			CAST(DATEADD(YEAR , 1 , HIREDATE) AS DATE) AS DATE_EAR_2
			FROM EMP_DATA ;

		SELECT 
			HIREDATE,
			CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENT_DATE,
			DATEDIFF(MONTH , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENT_MONTH,
			DATEDIFF(DAY , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFETENT_DAY
			FROM EMP_DATA

	SELECT 
		HIREDATE,
			CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE),
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENT_YEAR,
			DATEDIFF(MONTH , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENT_MONTH,
			DATEDIFF(DAY , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFETENT_DAY,
				CASE 
					WHEN DATEDIFF(DAY,HIREDATE,GETDATE()) > 1000 THEN 'DATEISOK'
					WHEN DATEDIFF(DAY, HireDate, GETDATE()) BETWEEN 500 AND 1000 THEN 'SALARY_INCRESED'
					WHEN DATEDIFF(MONTH , HireDate ,GETDATE()) > 5 THEN 'MONTH_IS_HIGH'
					WHEN DATEDIFF(YEAR , HireDate , GETDATE()) >=10  THEN ' NEW_JOINING_PERSONS'
					ELSE 'NOTOK'
				END
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE HireDate >= '2024-05-01'
	ORDER BY HireDate DESC 

	SELECT 
		HIREDATE,
		GETDATE()
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE HIREDATE >= CAST(GETDATE()AS DATE) AND HIREDATE < DATEADD (DAY , 10, CAST (GETDATE() AS DATE )) 

	SELECT *
	FROM EMP_DATA
	WHERE HireDate >= DATEADD(MONTH, DATEDIFF(MONTH, 0, GETDATE()), 0)

	USE TESTING ;
	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS 
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS 
	SELECT * FROM PROJECTS 

	SELECT * 
	FROM DEPARTMENTS 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES 
	ON DEPARTMENTS.DEPARTMENTID = EMPLOYEES.DEPARTMENTID

	SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'

		SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE DepartmentCode LIKE '%IT%' AND Salary > 75000 AND HireDate >= '2018-03-13' AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' 

		SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Email
	FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE DepartmentCode LIKE '%IT%' AND Salary > 50000 AND HireDate >= '2018-03-13' AND Status = 'active'


SELECT 
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYESS,
		D.DepartmentCode,
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Bonus,
		E.Email,
		E.Salary + E.BONUS AS TOTAL_SALARY_EMPLOYES,
		E.Salary + 10000 AS TOTAL_1000_INCRESED,
		E.Salary - 10000 AS TOTAL_1000_DECRESED,
		E.SALARY * 2 AS TOTAL_2_MULPTPLIES,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.Salary > 200000 THEN 'HIGH_SALARY' 
					ELSE 'LOW_SALARY'
			END AS TOTAL_SALARY_DETAILS,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.Bonus BETWEEN 15000 AND 50000 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE 'BONUS_IS_LESS'
			END AS BONUS_LEVEL,
			CASE 
					WHEN E.HIREDATE <= '2015-01-01' THEN 'VERY_OLD'
					WHEN E.HIREDATE BETWEEN '2015-01-01' AND '2020-01-01' THEN '5_YEARS_BEFORE'
					WHEN E.HIREDATE > '2020-01-01' THEN 'NEW_JOINING'
			END AS DATE_CHECKING,
		SUM(E.SALARY)  AS NEW_SALARY,
		MIN(E.SALARY)  AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(E.SALARY)  AS MAX_SALARY,
		AVG(E.SALARY)  AS AVG_SALARY
FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
WHERE GENDER = 'M' AND BONUS IS NOT NULL 
GROUP BY 
		D.DepartmentName, 
		D.DepartmentCode, 
		D.Location,
		D.Budget,
		D.IsActive,
		E.FirstName,
		E.LastName,
		E.Gender,
		E.HireDate,
		E.Salary,
		E.Bonus,
		E.Email
HAVING SUM(E.SALARY) > 150000 
ORDER BY E.SALARY DESC 


	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS D  
		INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID 
		INNER JOIN PROJECTS P
	ON D.DepartmentID = P.DepartmentID
		INNER JOIN EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS EP
	ON EP.ProjectID = P.ProjectID
		AND EP.ROLENAME = 'LEAD'


SELECT * , 
	E.SALARY + 1000 AS NEW_SALARY ,
	E.Salary - 1000 AS NEW_MINUS_SALARY,
	E.SALARY * 2 AS MULTIPLE_SALARY,
	E.SALARY / 10 AS DIVIDED_SALARY,
	E.Salary + E.Bonus AS SALARY_Bonus,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.DATEOFBIRTH BETWEEN '1990-01-01' AND '1995-01-01' THEN '35_AGE'
				ELSE 'BELOW_30'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.HireDate < '2015-01-01' THEN 'OLD_EMPLOYESS'
				WHEN E.HireDate BETWEEN '2015-01-01' AND '2020-01-01' THEN 'MIDDLE_EMPLOYEE'
				WHEN E.HireDate  > '2020-01-01' THEN 'NEW_EMPLOYEES'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.SALARY < 50000 THEN 'SALARY IS LOWER'
				WHEN E.Salary BETWEEN  50000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL_SALARY'
				WHEN E.Salary > 150000 THEN 'HIGH_SALARY'
		END,

		CASE 
				WHEN E.ExperienceYears < 3 THEN 'LOW_EXPERIENCE'
				WHEN E.ExperienceYears BETWEEN  3 AND 8 THEN 'HIGH'
				WHEN E.Salary > 8 THEN 'NOT_POSSIBLE'
		END

	FROM DEPARTMENTS  D  
			LEFT JOIN EMPLOYEES E
			ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
			LEFT JOIN PROJECTS P
			ON D.DepartmentID = P.DepartmentID
			LEFT JOIN EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS EP
			ON P.ProjectID = EP.ProjectID
	WHERE E.Gender = 'M'
	ORDER BY D.DepartmentID DESC 


	SELECT COUNT (*) , DepartmentName FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	GROUP BY DepartmentName
	HAVING COUNT (*)  = 3 
	ORDER BY DepartmentName DESC 

	
	SELECT 
		D.DepartmentCode, 
		D.DepartmentName,
		D.Location , 
		CAST(D.CreatedDate AS DATE) AS DATE_FORMAT,
		CONCAT(E.FIRSTNAME , E.LASTNAME) AS TOTAL_NAME ,
				CASE 
					WHEN E.Gender = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
					WHEN E.Gender = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		YEAR(E.DATEOFBIRTH) AS YEAR_ONLY,
		YEAR(E.HireDate) AS YEAR_ONLY_HIREDATE,
				CASE 
					WHEN ManagerID IS NULL THEN '0'
					ELSE ManagerID
				END AS UPDATE_MANAGER_ID , 

				CASE 
					WHEN BONUS IS NULL THEN '0'
					ELSE BONUS
				END AS BONUS_VALUE_UPDATED ,

				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 50000 AND 150000  THEN 'MEDIMUM'
					WHEN SALARY > 150000 THEN 'HIGH'
				END AS UPDATED_SALARY,
			   SUM(E.SALARY) OVER (
								PARTITION BY E.DepartmentID
							) AS TOTAL_SALARY
		FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
			RIGHT JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
			ON D. DepartmentID = E. DepartmentID 
		WHERE Gender  = 'M' AND LOCATION = 'MUMBAI'
	

	SELECT COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_COUNT, DepartmentName  FROM DEPARTMENTS D 
	FULL OUTER JOIN EMPLOYEES E 
	ON D.DepartmentID = E.DepartmentID
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'
	GROUP BY DepartmentName
	HAVING COUNT(*) = 2

	-----------------------------------------------------------CROSS JOIN IT IS USED LESS IN DATA ENGINEERING --------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS D
	CROSS JOIN EMPLOYEES E
	-----------------------------------------------------------SELF JOIN DATA ENGINEERING ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
	---Find Employees Who Earn More Than Their Manager -------------------
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES

	SELECT * FROM PROJECTS

	SELECT 
	E.FIRSTNAME AS EMPLOYEENAME ,
	E.SALARY AS EMPLYEE_SALARY,
	M.FIRSTNAME AS MANAGERNAME ,
	M.Salary AS MANAGER_SALARY
	FROM EMPLOYEES E 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES M 
	ON E.MANAGERID = M.EMPLOYEEID 
	WHERE E.Salary > M.Salary AND E.Status = 'ACTIVE' 
	ORDER BY M.FirstName
	

	SELECT 
	M.FIRSTNAME AS MANAGER_NAME,
	COUNT(E.EmployeeID) AS TOTAL_MANAGER_COUNT
	FROM EMPLOYEES E 
	INNER JOIN EMPLOYEES M
	ON E.ManagerID = M.EmployeeID
	GROUP BY M.FirstName
	HAVING COUNT(E.EmployeeID) > 2;

			UPPER(DEPARTMENT) AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
				CASE
					WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'SALARY_IS_LOW' 
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE'HIGH'
				END AS NEW_DATA
		FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
UNION
		SELECT 
			UPPER(EMPLOYEENAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(DEPARTMENT) AS NEW_DEPARTMENT, 
				CASE
					WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'SALARY_IS_LOW' 
					WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 150001 THEN 'NORMAL'
					ELSE'HIGH'
				END AS NEW_DATA
		FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
) 

	SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
	WHERE NEW_DATA = 'NORMAL';

WITH DATA_CHK AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

		UNION 

		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
	),

	FINAL_OUTPUT AS 

	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENT,
			COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES,
			SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
			MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
			FROM DATA_CHK
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
			HAVING COUNT(*) = 2
	),

	FINAL_OUTPUT_VEERA AS

	(
		SELECT * FROM FINAL_OUTPUT 
		WHERE TOTAL_SALARY < 250000
	)

		SELECT * FROM FINAL_OUTPUT_VEERA
		WHERE Department = 'HR'

	WITH DATA_CHK AS

		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR

		UNION ALL

		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
	)

		SELECT

			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,

			MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
			FROM DATA_CHK

WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM ACTIVE_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM NORTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM SELECTED_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
		UNION ALL
		SELECT * FROM SOUTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR
	),
SECOND_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA
		WHERE Department IN ('IT' ,'SALES' , 'SUPPORT')
	),
THIRD_DATA AS 
	(

		SELECT * FROM SECOND_DATA
		WHERE EmployeeID >= 105
	),
FINAL_DATA AS 
	(	
		SELECT * , 
			CASE
				WHEN Salary < 100000 THEN 'LOW'
				ELSE 'HIGH'
			END AS SALARY_CATEGORY
		FROM THIRD_DATA
	),


FINAL_DATA_FINAL AS
		
	(	
		SELECT 
		Department,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM FINAL_DATA
		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT 
	)
		
		SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA_FINAL
		WHERE DEPARTMENT = 'IT'

---------------------------------------------------------------------- SUB QUERY WITH FROM LOCATION -----------------------------------------------------
		
	SELECT * FROM 
		(	
			SELECT 
				CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
				GENDER,
				EMAIL,
				STATUS,
				PHONE,
				SALARY ,
					CASE 
						WHEN Salary < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
						WHEN Salary BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
						WHEN Salary BETWEEN 150000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
						ELSE 'HIGH'
					END AS SALARY_TYPE
			FROM EMPLOYEES
		) AS DT
	WHERE SALARY_TYPE = 'HIGH'

	
	SELECT * FROM 
			
		(
			SELECT 
				DepartmentID,
				COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYESS,
				SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_EMP_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES
			GROUP BY DepartmentID
		) AS T
	WHERE T.TOTAL_EMP_SALARY BETWEEN 200000 AND 300000
	ORDER BY T.TOTAL_EMP_SALARY DESC 
	
	WITH FINAL_DATA	AS 	
			(	
				SELECT 
					CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
					GENDER,
					EMAIL,
					STATUS,
					PHONE,
					SALARY ,
						CASE 
							WHEN Salary < 50000 THEN 'LOW'
							WHEN Salary BETWEEN 100000 AND 150000 THEN 'NORMAL'
							WHEN Salary BETWEEN 150000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
							ELSE 'HIGH'
						END AS SALARY_TYPE
				FROM EMPLOYEES
			)
	SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
	WHERE SALARY_TYPE = 'LOW'

WITH FINAL_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT 
				DEPARTMENT,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMP_DATA)
					THEN 'GOOD'
					ELSE 'BAD'
					END AS NEW_DATA,
				CASE 
					WHEN BONUS >
						(SELECT AVG(BONUS) FROM EMP_DATA)
					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE 'NOT_OK'
					END AS NEW_DATA_1	
		FROM EMP_DATA
	)
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
WHERE NEW_DATA = 'GOOD' AND NEW_DATA_1 = 'OK'

	SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , ES.FIRSTNAME , ES.LASTNAME) ,
			CASE
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			END AS NEW_GENDER,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.LOCATION
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES
	WHERE SALARY > 
		(	
			SELECT 
				AVG(ES2.SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
			WHERE ES.DEPARTMENTID = ES2.DEPARTMENTID
		)


	SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , ES.FIRSTNAME , ES.LASTNAME) ,
			CASE
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
				WHEN ES.GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			END AS NEW_GENDER,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.LOCATION
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES
	WHERE SALARY <
		(	
			SELECT 
				SUM(ES2.BONUS) AS TOTAL_SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
			WHERE ES.DEPARTMENTID = ES2.DEPARTMENTID
		)
