-----------------------------------------------------------------------------MULTI ROW SUB QUERY --------------------------------------------

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			AGE
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE DepartmentID IN  --------------- IN USED FOR MULTIPLE ROWS DATA ----------------IN MENAS ONLY PARTICULAR CITY---------------------------
	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
	)
	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			AGE
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE DepartmentID NOT IN  --------------- NOT IN USED FOR MULTIPLE ROWS DATA ----------------------- NOT IN USED FOR EXCPT THAT CITY --------------
	(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
	)

		SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY > ANY ------------------------------------------ANY IS USED Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery--------------
	(
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)

	SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY < SOME ------------------------------------------SOME  IS USED Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery--------------
	(	
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)
	---------------------BOTH SOME AND ANY IS SAME LOGIC ONLY /////Condition must be true for at least one value returned by the subquery===================
	SELECT 
			EmployeeID,
			SALARY,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			GENDER FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY < ALL -------------------------Condition must be true for every value returned by the subquery-----------------------------
	(	
		SELECT SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EmployeeID = 1099
	)

---------------=====================================================FROM SUBQUERY==========================================--------------------------

	SELECT *
		FROM 
			(
				SELECT
				UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
				CASE 
						WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
						WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
				END AS NEW_GENDER,
				YEAR(HIREDATE)AS NEW_YEAR
				FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
			)AS S
	WHERE NEW_GENDER = 'FEMALE'
		
	
	SELECT * FROM 
			(
				SELECT 
					UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
					CASE 
						WHEN SALARY < 100000 THEN 'NOTOK'
						WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 200000 THEN 'OK'
						ELSE 'HIGH'
					END AS NEW_SALARY,
					CASE 
						WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
						WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
					END AS NEW_GENDER,
					YEAR (HIREDATE) AS NEW_YEAR,
					SUBSTRING(EMAIL , 0 , 10) AS NEW_MAIL_ID
					FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
			) AS NEW
	WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'OK'

	---------------=====================================================SELECT  SUBQUERY==========================================--------------------------		
	SELECT 
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS  NEW_LAST_NAME,
		SALARY,
		LOCATION,
				(SELECT SUM(SALARY) AS SUM_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT MAX(SALARY) AS  MAX_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ),
				(SELECT COUNT(*)  AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY )
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI';

------------------------------------------------------------------- 25-08-2026---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SELECT
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		CASE
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER,
		CASE
			WHEN DEPARTMENTID < 20 THEN 'OLD'
			WHEN DEPARTMENTID BETWEEN 20 AND 40 THEN 'NORMAL'
			ELSE 'NEW'
		END AS NEW_DEPARTMENTID,
		(
			SELECT AVG(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS AVG_SALARY,

		(
			SELECT SUM(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS SUM_SALARY,
		(
			SELECT MIN(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS MIN_SALARY,

		(
			SELECT MAX(SALARY)
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		) AS MAX_SALARY , 

		SALARY - (SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY) AS DETECTION_SALARY , 

		CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY) THEN 'OK'
				ELSE'NOTOK' 
		END NEW_SALARY_DETAILS,
		DATEDIFF (YEAR , HireDate , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_YEAR
  
FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY;


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------NSESTED SUBQUERY LOGICS ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------Find employees who work in departments located in Chennai.-----------------------------------
 
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN 
		(SELECT DEPARTMENTID  FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI')

--------------------Find employees whose salary is greater than the average salary of employees working in Chennai departments.---------------------------------------

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT AVG(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)
	
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT MIN(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT MAX(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)

	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary < 
		(
			SELECT SUM(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE'))
		)
--------------------------Find employees working in departments that have employees, where those departments are located in Chennai.------------------------------------------------
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN 
		(	
			SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID HAVING  COUNT(*) >= 1 AND DEPARTMENTID IN 
				(SELECT DEPARTMENTID  FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI')
		)

	
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE Salary >  
		(
			SELECT AVG(Salary) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
				(
					SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE') AND DEPARTMENTID IN 
						(
							SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE Status = 'COMPLETED' AND ProjectID IN 
								(
									SELECT ProjectID FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE Role IN ('DATA ENGINEER' , 'ANALYST' , 'MANAGER' , 'TESTER' , 'MANAGER') AND ProjectID IN 
										(
											SELECT ProjectID FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY WHERE StartDate BETWEEN '2024-01-01' AND '2025-01-01'
										)
								)
						)
				)
		);


 (
	SELECT * FROM 	
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			SALARY,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)

					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE'NOTOK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	) T
	WHERE NEW_LOGIC = 'NOTOK' ) 

	
	SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			SALARY,
				CASE 
					WHEN SALARY > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)
					THEN 'OK'
					ELSE'NOTOK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC,
				CASE 
					WHEN Bonus > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(Bonus) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY WHERE DepartmentID IN 
								(
									SELECT DepartmentID FROM DEPARTMENTS WHERE LOCATION IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
								)
						)
					THEN 'BONUS_OK'
					ELSE'BONUS_NOT_OK'
				END AS NEW_LOGIC
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	
-------------===================================================correlated Sub Query=======================================================--------------------------------------------------------------------

	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary > 
				(
					SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)


		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary < 
				(
					SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)


		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE ES.Salary > 
				(
					SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				)

		
		SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
			WHERE
				(
					SELECT COUNT(*) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2 ---------------------------------- CORRELATED SUB QUERY WILL RUN BASED ON OUTER QUERY-----------------------------------------
					WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID 
				) > 1

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------	Correlated Subqueries WITH CASE -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.SALARY,
			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'ABOVE_DEPARTMENT_SALARY' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'EQUAL' 
				ELSE 'NOT_EQUAL'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC,

			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT MAX(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'OK' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT MAX(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'TK' 
				ELSE 'PK'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC,

			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT MIN(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'MIN_OK' 
				WHEN SALARY = 
					(
						SELECT MIN(SALARY) 
						FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2 
						WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DepartmentID
					)
				THEN 'MIN_NOT' 
				ELSE 'HIGH'
			END AS NEW_LOGIC	
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------Multiple Correlated Subqueries-----------------------------------------------------------------------			
	SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE,
			(
				SELECT  AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_AVG_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_SUM_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MIN_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MAX(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MAX_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT COUNT(*) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MAX_SALARY 

	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES 
	
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------Multiple Correlated Subqueries + CASE------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 SELECT * FROM 

 (SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE,
			(
				SELECT  AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_AVG_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT SUM(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_SUM_SALARY ,
			(
				SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES2
				WHERE ES2.DepartmentID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
			) AS DEPARTMENT_MIN_SALARY ,

		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'OK'
			ELSE 'NOT_OK'
		END AS AVG_NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'VEERAMANI'
			WHEN SALARY = 
				(
					SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'EUQAL_AVERAGE'
			ELSE 'NOT_QUAL'
		END AS NEW_LOGICS,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY >
				(
					 SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					 WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'MIN_SALARY'
			WHEN SALARY = 
				(
					SELECT MIN(SALARY) FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES2
					WHERE ES2.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
				)
			THEN 'CHECKINH'
			ELSE 'SALARY_OK'
		END AS MIN_SALARY_CHECKING
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY ES ) T
	WHERE T.AVG_NEW_SALARY = 'OK'
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------EXISTS   Beginner Level--------------------------------------------
	
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.GENDER,
		ES.SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	    WHERE EXISTS          ----------------------------------------EXISTS checks whether at least one matching row exists in the subquery.----------------
	(
		SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
		WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
	)

	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.GENDER,
		ES.SALARY
		FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	    WHERE EXISTS 
	(
		SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
		WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
		AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M' ----------------------------------------WHERE + Multiple Conditions---------------------------------------
	)
---------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE EXISTS CONDITION WITH AND -------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
			UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FRIST_NAME,
			UPPER(ES.LASTNAME)  AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
			ES.GENDER,
			ES.SALARY
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES
	WHERE EXISTS  -------------------------FIRST EXIT CONFITION -----------------------------------------------
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS 
			WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID 
			AND DS.LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
		)
	 AND EXISTS  ---------------------------------------------SECOND EXITS CONDITION ---------------------------------------
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE ES.EMPLOYEEID = EPS.EMPLOYEEID
			
		)

----------------------------------------------MULTIPLE EXSITS CONDITION WITH AND CHECKING PURPOSE ------------------------------------------------------
SELECT * FROM 
	(
		SELECT 
			UPPER(E.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
			UPPER(E.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LASTNAME,
			E.SALARY,
			E.AGE AS NEW_AGE
		    FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS E
			WHERE E.SALARY > 100000
		AND EXISTS 
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS D
				WHERE D.DEPARTMENTID = E.DEPARTMENTID 
				AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' 
			)
		AND EXISTS 
			(
				SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS P
				WHERE P.EMPLOYEEID = E.EMPLOYEEID
			)

		)tS 
WHERE tS.NEW_AGE > 35
----------------------------------------------------NON EXSITS CONDITION CHECKING ------------------------------------------------------------------
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES 
	WHERE NOT EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE EPS.EMPLOYEEID = ES.EMPLOYEEID
		)

	
	SELECT 
		UPPER(ES.FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_FIRSTNAME,
		UPPER(ES.LASTNAME) AS NEW_LAST_NAME,
		ES.SALARY,
		ES.AGE
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS ES 
	WHERE  EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
			WHERE DS.DEPARTMENTID = ES.DEPARTMENTID
		)
	AND NOT EXISTS 
		(
			SELECT 1 FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS EPS
			WHERE EPS.EMPLOYEEID = ES.EMPLOYEEID
		)


----------------------------------------------CTE ---- COMMON TABLE EXPRESSION ----------------------------------------------
----- WHAT IS CTE --- IT IS COMMON TABLE EXPRESSION ------
----A CTE is a named temporary result set that you define using WITH and then use in the query immediately following it.------------------------
 
 WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
 (
	SELECT	
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS LAST_NAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS FULL_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY < 70000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 70000  AND 100000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN BONUS < 20000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN BONUS BETWEEN 20000  AND 40000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_BONUS,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS ONLY_YEAR,
		LOCATION,
		AGE 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA  WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'MEDIUM'

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE WITH WHERE CONDITION LOGICS--------------------------------------------------------------------------
 WITH FIRST_DATA AS 
 (
	SELECT	
		UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
		UPPER(LASTNAME) AS LAST_NAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS FULL_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN GENDER = 'F' THEN 'FEMALE'
			WHEN GENDER = 'M' THEN 'MALE'
		END AS NEW_GENDER_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY < 70000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 70000  AND 100000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN BONUS < 20000 THEN 'LOW'
			WHEN BONUS BETWEEN 20000  AND 40000 THEN 'MEDIUM'
			ELSE 'HIGH'
		END AS NEW_BONUS,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS ONLY_YEAR,
		LOCATION,
		AGE 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
	WHERE Location = 'CHENNAI'
)
SELECT * FROM FIRST_DATA  WHERE NEW_SALARY = 'MEDIUM';

	FROM EMP_DATA

SELECT * FROM DATA_CHECKING ;

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE WITH SELECTED COLUMNS --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH CAL_COLUMN AS 
(
	SELECT 
		UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' ,FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF'), 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER_DIFFERENT_FORMAT,--------------------WIHTOUT CASE I USED CHOOSE AND CHAIRINDEX FOR CONDITION 
		IIF (GENDER = 'M', 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') NEW_IF_TYPE,-----------------------------------------------WITHOUT CASE I USED IIF CONDITION FOR THIS SECTIONS
		TRIM(DEPARTMENT) AS SAPCE_REMOVED_DEPARTMENT,
		TRIM(CITY) AS NEW_CITY,
		TRIM(STATE) AS NEW_STATE,
		YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
		SALARY + 10000 AS NEW_INCREMENT_SALARY,
		SALARY - 10000 AS NEW_DECREMENT_SALARY,
		SALARY + BONUS AS NEW_SALARY_BONUS,
		SALARY / 10 AS DIVIDED_SALARY,
		SALARY * 2 AS MULTIPLE_SALARY,
		SALARY % 100 AS MODULE_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
)
SELECT * FROM CAL_COLUMN WHERE NEW_CITY = 'CHENNAI'	
(
	SELECT 
		DEPARTMENT,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
-----------------------------------------------------------CTE + GROUP BY CHECKING --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH GROUPBY_SUMMARY AS 
(
	SELECT 
		DEPARTMENT,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
		SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
		AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
		MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
		MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
		FROM EMP_DATA
		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
)
SELECT * FROM GROUPBY_SUMMARY;

-----------------------------------------------------------CTE + GROUP BY CHECKING + HAVING CHECKING  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH HAVING_CHECKING AS 

		GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
		HAVING MAX(SALARY) < 150000 AND DEPARTMENT IN ('IT' , 'HR' , 'QA')
)
SELECT * FROM HAVING_CHECKING;

-----------------------------------------------------------Multiple CTE's cHEKING   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
--------------------------THIS IS CALLLED AS CTE CHAINING -------------------------------------------
WITH 
	MULTIPLE_1 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEES 
			WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
		),

	MULTIPLE_2 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
			WHERE LOCATION  IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
			AND GENDER = 'M'
		),
	MULTIPLE_3 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMP_DATA 
			WHERE City  IN ('CHENNAI' , 'PUNE')
			AND GENDER = 'M'
			AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
		),
	MULTIPLE_4 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM EMPLOYEE_PROJECTS_SUBQUERY 
			WHERE ROLE  IN ('TESTER' , 'ANALYST')
		),
	MULTIPLE_5 AS 
		(
			SELECT 
				DEPARTMENT,
				COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
				SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
				AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
				MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
				MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
				FROM EMP_DATA
				GROUP BY DEPARTMENT
				HAVING MAX(SALARY) < 150000 AND DEPARTMENT IN ('IT' , 'HR' , 'QA')	
		),
	MULTIPLE_6 AS 
		(
			SELECT  * FROM SOUTH_EMPLOYEES_SETOPERATOR 
			WHERE Department  IN ('IT' , 'R&D')
		)
SELECT * FROM MULTIPLE_6 ;


--------------------------------------------------------CTE + JOIN-----------------------------------------
--------------------------------------------------------Very important for Data Engineering.-----------------------------------------
WITH JOIN_TESTING AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE' 
	)
					SELECT 
					UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , EP.FIRSTNAME , EP.LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
					CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(EP.GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
					YEAR(EP.DATEOFBIRTH) AS YEAR_ONLY,
					YEAR(EP.HIREDATE) AS YEAR_HIREDATE,
					EP.SALARY,
					EP.BONUS,
					EP.STATUS,
					DP.DEPARTMENTNAME,
					DP.LOCATION,
					YEAR(DP.CREATEDDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
					PS.PROJECTNAME,
					PS.BUDGET
					FROM JOIN_TESTING AS EP
						INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS AS DP
						ON EP.DEPARTMENTID = DP.DEPARTMENTID
						INNER JOIN PROJECTS AS PS
						ON PS.DEPARTMENTID = EP.DEPARTMENTID
					WHERE BONUS IS NOT NULL


------------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE CTE + JOIN------------------------------------------
WITH EMPLOYESS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS IN  ('ACTIVE' , 'INACTIVE')
	),
DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS 
		WHERE LOCATION IN ( 'CHENNAI' , 'PUNE' , 'MUMBAI' , 'DELHI')
	),
PROJECTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM PROJECTS 
		WHERE PROJECTSTATUS = 'ACTIVE'
	)
				
				SELECT 
					FIRSTNAME,
					LASTNAME,
					SALARY,
					GENDER
					FROM EMPLOYESS_DATA AS E 
					INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS D
					ON E.DEPARTMENTID = D.DEPARTMENTID
					INNER JOIN PROJECTS_DATA AS P
					ON E.DEPARTMENTID = P.DEPARTMENTID ;


------------------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE CTE + JOIN + GROUP BY ------------------------------------------

WITH EMPLOYESS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES 
		WHERE STATUS IN  ('ACTIVE' , 'INACTIVE')
	),
DEPARTMENTS_DATA AS 
	(
		SELECT *
			FROM DEPARTMENTS  
			
	)
		SELECT 
			DD.Location
			FROM EMPLOYESS_DATA AS ED
			INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS_DATA DD
			ON ED.DepartmentID = DD.DepartmentID
			WHERE GENDER = 'M'
			GROUP BY LOCATION ;

(
		SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE SALARY > ------------------------SINGLE LINE SUBQUERY WITH CTE GREATER COMPARISON 
			(
				SELECT AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY  
			) 
		        AND  DEPARTMENTID IN 
			(
				SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
				WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
				AND MANAGERID IS NOT NULL 
			)

)
SELECT * FROM SINGLE_SUBQUERY_CTE;

	SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM SINGLE_DATA

-----------MULTI LINE SUB QUERY TESTING ------------------- IN SUB QUERY -----------------------------------------------

WITH MULTIPLE_TESTING_IN AS 
(	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT  * FROM MULTIPLE_TESTING_IN 
	WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN --------------------------MULTIPLE SUB QUERY CTE  , THIS QUERY IS JOINING THE ABOVE QUERY AND BELOW QUERY 

(	
	SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
	WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
)

-----------MULTI LINE SUB QUERY TESTING ------------------- NOT IN SUB QUERY -----------------------------------------------


WITH MULTIPLE_TESTING_NOT_IN AS 
(	
	SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT  * FROM MULTIPLE_TESTING_NOT_IN 
	WHERE DEPARTMENTID NOT IN --------------------------MULTIPLE SUB QUERY CTE  , THIS QUERY IS JOINING THE ABOVE QUERY AND BELOW QUERY 

(	
	SELECT DEPARTMENTID FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
	WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
)
----------------------------------------------- CTE WITH ANY QUERY CHECKING ----------------------------------------------
WITH ANY_CTE_CHEKING AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTID,
			EMPLOYEEID,
			SALARY,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
		SELECT * FROM ANY_CTE_CHEKING
		WHERE SALARY > ANY  -------------- MULTIPLE SUBQUERY WITH CTE AND ANY ------ ANY MEANS ALL THE RECORDS WILL COME ABOVE 1099 EMPLOYEE ID RECORDS
(
		SELECT SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1099'
)

			DEPARTMENTID,

			SALARY,

			IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
			IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
			IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
			YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
			DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
			LOCATION
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
		SELECT * FROM ANY_CTE_CHEKING
		WHERE SALARY > SOME  -------------- MULTIPLE SUBQUERY WITH CTE AND SOME ------ 
(
		SELECT SALARY FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1099'
)

WITH ALL_CTE_CHEKING AS

			DEPARTMENTID,
			EMPLOYEEID,
			SALARY,
			UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,

		WHERE EMPLOYEEID = '1011'

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------- 27-08-2026 ----------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + SCALAR SUB QUERY + SELECT LOCATION  ----------------------------------------------
WITH SCALAR_CTE_DATA_SELECT_LOCATION AS 
(
			SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
			SELECT 
					UPPER(CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
					CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF') , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
					IIF(GENDER = 'M' , 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') AS IF_LOGIC_GENDER,
					IIF(SALARY > 100000 , 'OK' , 'NOT_OK') AS NEW_SALARY,
					IIF(BONUS >= 50000 , 'BONUS_OK' , 'BONUS_NOT_OK') AS BONUS_VALUE,
					YEAR(HIREDATE) AS YEAR_ONLY,
					DATEDIFF(YEAR , HIREDATE , GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
					DATEADD(YEAR , 10 , GETDATE()) AS ADDED_DATE,
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY)  -------------- SCALAR SUBQUERY WITH SELECTION LOCTION ONLY ONE DATA CAN ENTER INSIDE THE QUERY CALLED AS A SCALAR SUB QUERY  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_AVG
						),
						(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY)  -------------- SCALAR SUBQUERY WITH SELECTION LOCTION ONLY ONE DATA CAN ENTER INSIDE THE QUERY CALLED AS A SCALAR SUB QUERY  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MIN
						),
							(
							SELECT MAX(SALARY)  -------------- SCALAR SUBQUERY WITH SELECTION LOCTION ONLY ONE DATA CAN ENTER INSIDE THE QUERY CALLED AS A SCALAR SUB QUERY  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MAX
						),
						(
							SELECT SUM(SALARY)  -------------- SCALAR SUBQUERY WITH SELECTION LOCTION ONLY ONE DATA CAN ENTER INSIDE THE QUERY CALLED AS A SCALAR SUB QUERY  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_SUM
						),
					LOCATION
					FROM SCALAR_CTE_DATA_SELECT_LOCATION;
---------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + SCALAR SUB QUERY + WHERE LOCATION  ----------------------------------------------	
WITH SCALAR_CTE_DATA_FROM_LOCATION AS 
(
			SELECT *
			FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)

				SELECT *
				FROM SCALAR_CTE_DATA_FROM_LOCATION 
				WHERE SALARY >
							(
								SELECT MIN(SALARY)
								FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
							) 
				AND SALARY >
							(
								SELECT AVG(SALARY)
								FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
							) 
				AND GENDER = 'M'

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING -------------------------------------------------------------

WITH CTE_DATA_SELECT_LOCATION AS 
(
			SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
			SELECT 

						DEPARTMENTID,
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) --------------   CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_AVG ,
						(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY)   -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
						)AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MIN,
							(
							SELECT MAX(SALARY)  -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
						)AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MAX,
						(
							SELECT SUM(SALARY)  -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
						)AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_SUM,
						(
							SELECT COUNT(*)  -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
							FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
						)AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_SUM,

						SALARY - (
									SELECT AVG(SALARY) -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
									FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
							) AS SALARY_AVG_SALARY ,

						SALARY + 
								(
									SELECT AVG(SALARY) -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
									FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
								) AS SALARY_AVG_SALARY ,
						
						SALARY + 
								(
									SELECT AVG(BONUS) -------------- CTE + SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
									FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
								) AS BONUS ,
					LOCATION
					FROM CTE_DATA_SELECT_LOCATION;

---------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + FROM Subquery--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WITH CTE_FROM_SUBQUERY_CHECKING AS 
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY 
		)
		
				SELECT * FROM 
				   (
							SELECT               ---------------------------------- SINGLE SELCT QUERY IN  FROM LOCATION AND CTE 
							DEPARTMENTID AS NEW_DEPARTMENT_NAME,
							AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
							MIN(SALARY) AS MAS_SALARY,
							MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
							FROM CTE_FROM_SUBQUERY_CHECKING
							GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID
					) AS T 

	WITH CTE_FROM_SUBQUERY_CHECKING AS  
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		)
			
		SELECT * FROM --------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY IN FROM SECTION -------------------
		  
		   (
				SELECT * FROM --------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY IN FROM SECTION -------------------
				(
					SELECT * FROM --------------------------------------------------MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY IN FROM SECTION -------------------
						   (
									SELECT
									DEPARTMENTID AS NEW_DEPARTMENT_NAME,
									AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
									MIN(SALARY) AS MAS_SALARY,
									MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY
									FROM CTE_FROM_SUBQUERY_CHECKING
									GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID
							) AS CHECKING_1
				) AS CHECKING_2
			) AS  CHECKING_3 
		WHERE CHECKING_3.AVG_SALARY > 200000


------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + Multiple Subqueries----------------------------------------------------X	
WITH MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION AS 	
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		)

				SELECT 
						DEPARTMENTID,
						SALARY,
						GENDER,
						AGE,
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) --------------   CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_AVG ,

						(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY)   --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MIN,

						(
							SELECT MAX(SALARY)  --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING  
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_MAX,

						(
							SELECT SUM(SALARY)  --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING 
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_SUM,

						(
							SELECT COUNT(*)  --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
						) AS SCALAR_SUB_QUERY_SUM,

						(
							SELECT COUNT(*) --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION AS D1
							WHERE D1.SALARY > 
								(
									SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
									MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION
								) 
						) AS AVG_DATA_MILTIPLE , 

					(
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION
						)  - 
						
						(
							SELECT SUM(SALARY) --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING.
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION
						) 
					) AS AVG_MINUS_SUM,

					(
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION
						)  +
						
						(
							SELECT SUM(SALARY) --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING.
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION
						) 
					) AS AVG_PLUS_SUM , 

					BONUS + 
					(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY)   --------------  CTE + MULTIPLE SELECT QUERY CHECKING
							FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION 
					) AS BONUS_MIN_PLUS_SALARY

				FROM MULTIPLE_SUBQUERY_WITH_CTE_FUNCTION;
					

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + Multiple NESTED Subqueries----------------------------------------------------X	

WITH MULTIPLE_NESTED_SUBQUERY AS 	
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		)

				SELECT * FROM MULTIPLE_NESTED_SUBQUERY AS MNS
				WHERE MNS.SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY)FROM MULTIPLE_NESTED_SUBQUERY AS MNS2
						WHERE  DEPARTMENTID IN 
							(
								SELECT D.DEPARTMENTID
									FROM DEPARTMENTS AS D
									WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND DEPARTMENTID IN 
									(
											SELECT DEPARTMENTID 
												FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS P 
												WHERE STATUS = 'ACTIVE' AND EmployeeID IN 
													(
														SELECT EmployeeID AS ES
															FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
															WHERE AGE > 50
													)
									)
							)
					)

	
	
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------CTE + CORRELATED SUBQUERY TESTING ----------------------------------------------------X	

WITH CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS 	
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		)
				SELECT 
					DEPARTMENTID,
					EMPLOYEEID,
					SALARY,
					AGE,
					GENDER
					FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS CS
					WHERE SALARY >
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
							FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS CS2
							WHERE CS2.DEPARTMENTID = CS.DEPARTMENTID -----CTE + CORRELATED SUBQUERY TESTING ----------------------------------------------------X	-THIS IS THE MAIN LINE FOR CORRELATED SUBQUERY
						)
					AND CS.SALARY >
						(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY) 
							FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS CS3
							WHERE CS3.DEPARTMENTID = CS.DEPARTMENTID-----------------CTE + CORRELATED SUBQUERY TESTING ----------------------------------------------------X	THIS IS THE MAIN LINE FOR CORRELATED SUBQUERY
						)
					AND CS.SALARY >
						(
							SELECT MIN(SALARY) + 100000
							FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS CS4
							WHERE CS4.DEPARTMENTID = CS.DEPARTMENTID---------------------CTE + CORRELATED SUBQUERY TESTING ----------------------------------------------------X	 THIS IS THE MAIN LINE FOR CORRELATED SUBQUERY 
						)



WITH CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS 	
		(
				SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		),
CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE_ANOTHER AS 
		(
				SELECT 
					DEPARTMENTID,
					COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES,
					AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
					MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
					MAX(SALARY) AS MX_SALARY,
					SUM(SALARY) AS SUM_SALARY
					FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE 
					GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID

		)
				SELECT
					CS.DEPARTMENTID,
					CS.EMPLOYEEID AS NEW_EMPLOYEEID,
					CS.SALARY,
					CS.AGE,
					CS.GENDER,
					DS.DEPARTMENTNAME,
					DS.LOCATION,
					DS.BUDGET,
					DS.ISACTIVE,
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) 
							FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE
						) - SALARY  AS NEW_SALARY,
						(
							SELECT MIN (BONUS) 
							FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE
						) +  BONUS  AS BONUS_PLUS_MINUM

					FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE AS CS
					INNER JOIN DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS DS
					ON CS.DEPARTMENTID = DS.DEPARTMENTID
					WHERE SALARY > 
						(
							SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM CORRELATED_SUBQUERY_CTE CS1
							WHERE CS1.DEPARTMENTID = CS.DEPARTMENTID
						)
					AND GENDER = 'M' 
									
				
	-----------------------------------------------CTE + EXISTS ---------------------------------------------------		
WITH EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS 

(
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT
		FIRSTNAME ,
		LASTNAME ,
		SALARY,
		AGE,
		GENDER ,
		LOCATION
		FROM EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS A 
		WHERE EXISTS  ---------------------------EXISTS WILL CHECK ANOTHE TABLE WHERE THE DATA AVAILABLE OR NOT IF MATCHED MEANS THOSE RECORD WILL COME TO THE OUTPUT 
			(
				SELECT 1 
				FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS B
				WHERE A.DEPARTMENTID = B.DEPARTMENTID
				AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
			)
		AND EXISTS ------------ THIS IS ANOTHER TABLE ADDED WITH EXISTS FUNCTION (IN THIS QUERY TOTALLY 3 TABLE CHECKED)
			(
				SELECT 1 
				FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS C
				WHERE A.DEPARTMENTID = C.DEPARTMENTID
				AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
			)

-----------------------------------------------CTE + NOT EXISTS ---------------------------------------------------		
WITH EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS 

(
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT
		FIRSTNAME ,
		LASTNAME ,
		SALARY,
		AGE,
		GENDER ,
		LOCATION
		FROM EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS A 
		WHERE NOT EXISTS  ---------------------------NOT EXISTS WILL CHECK ANOTHE TABLE WHERE THE DATA AVAILABLE OR NOT IF NOT MATCHED  THOSE RECORD WILL COME TO THE OUTPUT 
			(
				SELECT 1 
				FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY AS B
				WHERE A.DEPARTMENTID = B.DEPARTMENTID
				AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
			)

WITH EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS 

(
	SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
)
	SELECT
		FIRSTNAME ,
		LASTNAME ,
		SALARY,
		AGE,
		GENDER ,
		LOCATION
		FROM EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS A 
		WHERE NOT EXISTS --------NOT EXISTS WILL CHECK ANOTHE TABLE WHERE THE DATA AVAILABLE OR NOT IF NOT MATCHED  THOSE RECORD WILL COME TO THE OUTPUT 
			(
				SELECT 1 
					FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY AS B
					WHERE B.DEPARTMENTID = A.DEPARTMENTID
					AND  B.STATUS = 'COMPLETED'
			)
-------------------------------------------------------Multiple CTE + Subquery--------------------------------------------------------------

WITH EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS  ---------------- FIRST CTE ---------

		(
			SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		),
NEXT_CTE AS  ------------------ SECOND CTE ----------------
		(
			SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
		)

		
			SELECT *
				FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS A 
				WHERE SALARY >
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM 
						EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS B
						WHERE B.DEPARTMENTID IN 
							(
								SELECT C.DEPARTMENTID FROM 
									NEXT_CTE AS C
										WHERE C.LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
							)
					) 


-------------------------------------------------------Multiple CTE + Subquery--------------------------------------------------------------

WITH EXSITS_CTE_CHECKING AS  ---------------- FIRST CTE ---------

		(
			SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		),
NEXT_CTE AS  ------------------ SECOND CTE ----------------
		(
			SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
		),
NEXT_CTE_1 AS ------------------ THIRD CTE ----------------
		(
			SELECT * FROM PROJECTS_SUBQUERY
		)
			SELECT *
				FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS A 
				WHERE SALARY >
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY) FROM 
						EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY AS B
						WHERE B.DEPARTMENTID IN 
							(
								SELECT C.DEPARTMENTID FROM 
									NEXT_CTE AS C
										WHERE DEPARTMENTID IN 
											(
												SELECT D.DEPARTMENTID FROM 
													NEXT_CTE_1 AS D
														WHERE STATUS ='ACTIVE'
											)
							)
					) 


-------------------------------------------------------Multiple CTE + Correlated Subquery--------------------------------------------------------------

	WITH Multiple_CTE_Correlated_Subquery AS  ---------------- FIRST CTE ---------

		(
			SELECT * FROM EMPLOYEES_SUBQUERY
		),
NEXT_CTE AS  ------------------ SECOND CTE ----------------
		(
			SELECT * FROM DEPARTMENTS_SUBQUERY
		)
			SELECT 
				FIRSTNAME,
				LASTNAME,
				AGE,
				GENDER,
				SALARY,
				LOCATION FROM Multiple_CTE_Correlated_Subquery AS A 
				WHERE SALARY > 
					(
						SELECT AVG(SALARY)
						FROM Multiple_CTE_Correlated_Subquery AS B
						WHERE A.DEPARTMENTID = B.DEPARTMENTID
					)
				AND EXISTS 
					(
						SELECT 1 
						FROM NEXT_CTE AS C 
						WHERE C.DEPARTMENTID = A.DEPARTMENTID
					)

----------------------------------------WUNDOWS_FUNCTION -------------------------------------------
-------------------ROW_NUMBER() ------------------------------------------


SELECT
	EMPLOYEEID,
	FIRSTNAME,
	LASTNAME,
	GENDER,
	LOCATION,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ NORMAL QUERY WITHOUT PARTITION ()WITH SINGLE ORDER BY QUERY 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

SELECT
	EMPLOYEEID,
	FIRSTNAME,
	LASTNAME,
	GENDER,
	LOCATION,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	HIREDATE,
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY DESC , HIREDATE DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ NORMAL QUERY WITHOUT PARTITION ()WITH SINGLE ORDER BY QUERY 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

SELECT
	EMPLOYEEID,
	FIRSTNAME,
	LASTNAME,
	GENDER,
	LOCATION,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	HIREDATE,
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY DESC , EMPLOYEEID ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ Row Number with EmployeeID as Tie-Breaker (This is a good production habit when duplicate ordering values exist.)
	FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

SELECT
	EMPLOYEEID,
	FIRSTNAME,
	LASTNAME,
	GENDER,
	LOCATION,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	HIREDATE,
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ PARTITION BY WITH ORDER BY 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

SELECT
	EMPLOYEEID,
	FIRSTNAME,
	LASTNAME,
	GENDER,
	LOCATION,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	HIREDATE,
	ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION  , STATUS ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ MULTIPLE PARTITION BY WITH ORDER BY 
	FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;
--------------------------------------Filtering ROW_NUMBER()-------------------------------
WITH FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS AS 
	(
		SELECT
			EMPLOYEEID,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			STATUS,
			HIREDATE,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION  , STATUS ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ PARTITION BY FILTERING 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
	) 
SELECT * FROM FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS
WHERE NEW_ID < 2 AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE'

			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
