WITH FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS AS 
	(
		SELECT
			EMPLOYEEID,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			STATUS,
			HIREDATE,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ ORDER BY FILTERING ---- THIS IS THE LOWEST SALARY FILTERING (ASC) 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
	) 
SELECT * FROM FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS
WHERE NEW_ID <= 5


WITH MULTIPLE_CASE_WINDOWS_FUNCTION AS
(
		SELECT
			UPPER(FIRSTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			UPPER(LASTNAME) AS LAST_NEW_NAME,
			CHOOSE(CHARINDEX(GENDER , 'MF' ),'MALE', 'FEMALE') AS NEW_GENDER,
			IIF(GENDER =  'M' ,'MALE' , 'FEMALE' ) AS IIF_GENDER,
			CASE 
				WHEN GENDER  = 'M' THEN 'MALE'---------------------------------CASE WITH CTE AND WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
				ELSE 'FEMALE'
			END AS CASE_GENDER ,
			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY < 50000 THEN 'LOW_SALARY'
				WHEN SALARY BETWEEN 100000 AND 200000 THEN 'MEDIUM' ---------------------------------CASE WITH CTE AND WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
				ELSE'HIGH'
			END AS NEW_SALARY,
			CASE 
				WHEN BONUS < 10000 THEN 'LOW'
				WHEN BONUS BETWEEN 20000 AND 50000 THEN 'MEDIUM'---------------------------------CASE WITH CTE AND WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
				ELSE'HIGH'
			END AS NEW_BONUS, 
			YEAR(HIREDATE) - YEAR(GETDATE()) AS DIFFERENCE_DATE,
			LOCATION,
			ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS UNIQUE_ROWS
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
)
SELECT * FROM MULTIPLE_CASE_WINDOWS_FUNCTION
WHERE UNIQUE_ROWS  <= 10
AND LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
 
 -----------------------------------Advanced Data Engineering Pattern---------------------------------------------
 WITH ADVACNED_DATA_ENGINEERING_CTE AS 
	(
		SELECT
		* ,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY EMPLOYEEID ORDER BY  SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID   -------------- EMPLOYEEID IS PARTITION BY IN THIS TABLE THERE NO DUPLICATE ID SO WE COULD'T SEE THE DIFFERENCE 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS    -------------IF DUBLICATED EMPLOYEEID AVAILABLE THEN WE CAN SEE THE DIFFERENCE
	) 
SELECT * FROM ADVACNED_DATA_ENGINEERING_CTE
WHERE NEW_ID = 1 

------------Top N Per Group — Reusable Template---------------------------
------------This is the most important template:----------------------******************************-----VERY IMPORTANT TOPICS 

WITH FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS AS 
	(
		SELECT
			EW.EMPLOYEEID,
			EW.FIRSTNAME,
			EW.LASTNAME,
			EW.GENDER,
			EW.LOCATION,
			EW.SALARY,
			EW.STATUS,
			EW.HIREDATE,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ,    ------------------ ORDER BY FILTERING ---- THIS IS THE LOWEST SALARY FILTERING (ASC) 
			OW.PRODUCTCATEGORY,
			OW.ORDERDATE,
			OW.STATUS AS_ORDER_DATA
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS AS EW
			INNER JOIN ORDERS_WINDOWS AS OW  
			ON EW.EMPLOYEEID = OW.EMPLOYEEID
	) 
SELECT * FROM FILTERING_ROW_WINDOWS
WHERE NEW_ID  <= 5 
AND AS_ORDER_DATA = 'PENDING'

-------------------------------------------------------------RANK() FUNCTION ------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------Gives same rank for ties, but leaves gaps after the tie.------------------------------------

SELECT
	*,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ RANK ALL EMPLOYEES BY HIGH SALRY-------------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

SELECT
	*,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ RANK ALL EMPLOYEES BY LOW SALRY-------------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;
		
SELECT
	*,
	RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ Ranking Within Department-------------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;
------RANKING BY EMPLOYEE-------------------------
SELECT 
	EMPLOYEEID,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------Rank employees according to their total sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------Rank employees according to their AVG sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------Rank employees according to their MIN sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------Rank employees according to their MAX sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY EMPLOYEEID
-------RANKING BY DEPARTMENTS----------
SELECT 
	DEPARTMENTID,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their total sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their AVG sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MIN sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MAX sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENTID;

-------------RANKING BY DEPARTMENTNAME --------------
SELECT 
	DEPARTMENTNAME,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their total sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their AVG sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MIN sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MAX sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME;
-----------RANKING BY LOCATION --------	
SELECT 
	LOCATION,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their total sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their AVG sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MIN sales.
	RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MAX sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY LOCATION;
----WITH PARTITION BY ---------
SELECT 
	LOCATION,
	DEPARTMENTNAME,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION  ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their total sales.
	RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their AVG sales.
	RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MIN sales.
	RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------Rank DEPARTMENT according to their MAX sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY LOCATION ,DEPARTMENTNAME;

---------------RANK() WITH CTE QUERY ---------------------------------
WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME ,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATE,
			EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
			RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_RANK_FUNCTION 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION
WHERE NEW_RANK_FUNCTION <= 3 ;


------------------------------------RANK + CASE + CTE-----------------------------------------------

WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME ,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATE,
			EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
			CASE 
				WHEN 
					RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) = 1 THEN 'HIGHEST_SALARY' 
				WHEN
					RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) BETWEEN 2 AND 5 THEN 'MEDIUM_SALARY'
				ELSE 'LOW'
			END AS 'NEW_SALARY_TYPE'
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION ;

-------------------------------Advanced Business Query ---------------16. Highest Revenue Product Per Region----------------------------

WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SUM(SALARY) AS NEW_SALARY,
			RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS TOTAL_REVENU
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME , LOCATION 
)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION
WHERE TOTAL_REVENU <=3 
ORDER BY LOCATION DESC 

-------------------------------------------------------------DENSE_RANK() FUNCTION ------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------Gives same rank for ties, BUT NO GAP------------------------------------

SELECT
	*,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ DENSE_RANK ALL EMPLOYEES BY HIGH SALRY-------------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

SELECT
	*,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ DENSE_RANK ALL EMPLOYEES BY HIGH SALRY-----------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

SELECT
	*,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY ASC) AS NEW_ID ------------------ DENSE_RANK With Department-------------------
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

------DENSE_RANKING BY EMPLOYEE-------------------------
SELECT 
	EMPLOYEEID,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY,
	AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY EMPLOYEEID;
-----------------DENSE_RANKING BY DEPARTMENT --------------------
SELECT 
	DEPARTMENTNAME,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY,
	AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME;

-----------------DENSE_RANKING BY LOCATION --------------------

SELECT 
	LOCATION,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY,
	AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY LOCATION;

-----------------DENSE_RANKING WITH PARTITION BY AND ORDER  BY LOCATION --------------------
SELECT 
	LOCATION,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
	SUM(SALARY) AS TOTAL_SALARY,
	MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY,
	MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY,
	AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY,
	DENSE_RANK() OVER( PARTITION BY LOCATION  ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_FUNCTION,-------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY AVG (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_AVG,------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY MIN (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MIN,----------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY MAX (SALARY) DESC) AS RANK_MAX--------------------------------DENSE_Rank employees according to their total sales.
	FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	GROUP BY LOCATION;
--------------------------CTE + DENSE_RANK _ TOP FIND THE TOP 3 SALARY -------------------
WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME ,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATE,
			EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
			DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_RANK_FUNCTION --------------SINGLE PARTITION TYPE 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION
WHERE NEW_RANK_FUNCTION <= 3 ;

----------------CTE + DENSE_RANK _ TO FIND THE TOP 3 SALARY USING DEPARTMENTNAME AND LOCATION PARTITION -------------------------------
WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME ,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATE,
			EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
			DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME , LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_RANK_FUNCTION --------------MULTIPLE  PARTITION TYPE 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION
WHERE NEW_RANK_FUNCTION <= 3 ;

----------------CTE + DENSE_RANK _ TO FIND THE TOP 3 SALARY USING DEPARTMENTNAME AND LOCATION PARTITION -------------------------------
---------------------------CTE + DENSE_RANK() + CASE -----------------------------------------
WITH RANK_FUNCTION AS 
	(
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME ,
			LASTNAME,
			AGE,
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATE,
			EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
			DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME , LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS NEW_RANK_FUNCTION,--------------MULTIPLE  PARTITION TYPE 
			CASE 
				WHEN 
					DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) < 2 THEN 'HIGH_SALARY'
				WHEN 
					DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) BETWEEN 3 AND 5  THEN 'MEDIUM_SALARY'
				ELSE 'LOW_SALARY'
			END AS NEW_SALARY_TYPE , 
			CASE 
				WHEN 
					DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY BONUS DESC) < 2 THEN 'HIGH_BONUS'
				WHEN 
					DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY BONUS DESC) BETWEEN 3 AND 5  THEN 'MEDIUM_BONUS'
				ELSE 'LOW_BONUS'
			END AS NEW_BONUS_TYPE
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
	)
SELECT * FROM RANK_FUNCTION
WHERE NEW_RANK_FUNCTION <= 3 ;

------------Advanced — Location Top 3 Departments---------------------------------
WITH ADVANCED_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		LOCATION,
		SUM(SALARY) AS NEW_SALARY,
		DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SUM(SALARY) DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
		GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME , LOCATION 
)
SELECT * FROM ADVANCED_FUNCTION 
WHERE LOACTION_WISE_DATA <= 3 
ORDER BY LOCATION , DEPARTMENTNAME DESC ;

-----------------Advanced — Top Salary Level + Employee Count----------------------------------

WITH ADVANCED_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		SALARY,
		DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
SELECT 
	DEPARTMENTNAME,
	SALARY,
	COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES
	FROM ADVANCED_FUNCTION
	WHERE LOACTION_WISE_DATA <= 3 
	GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME, SALARY 
	ORDER BY SALARY DESC 

-------------------------------------------------------------NTILE() FUNCTION ------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------NTILE() divides the rows into a specified number of approximately equal groups (buckets).----------

-----------BUCKET DEFENITION ----------------- IF WHAT SHOULD I MENTION IN BUCKET LIST THAT SOULD BE EACH BUCKET REMAINDER ROW'S WILL ADDED FROM THE EVERY FIRST BUCKET 

(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		SALARY,
		NTILE(4) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 4 GROUPS MEANS 4 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO : 25+25+25+25)------------- FROM 26 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)

(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		SALARY,
		NTILE(2) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)

(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		SALARY,
		NTILE(30) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 30 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
-----------------------------HIGHEST SALARY AND LOWEST SALARY -----------------------------------
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		SALARY,
		NTILE(80) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
-----------------------------HIGHEST SALARY AND LOWEST SALARY -----------------------------------
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		BONUS,
		NTILE(80) OVER (ORDER BY BONUS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)

-----------------------------TO FIND THE EXPREIENCE HIGHEST TO LOWEST---- -----------------------------------
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)

----------------------------NTILE() WITH PARTITION BY_ DEPARTMENT  -----------------------------------------
WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(15) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
SELECT  * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION
WHERE DEPARTMENTNAME = 'SALES' ;

----------------------------NTILE() WITH PARTITION BY _ LOCATION  -----------------------------------------
WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
SELECT  * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION;

----------------------------NTILE() WITH PARTITION BY _ LOCATION _ HIGHEST BUCKET  -----------------------------------------
WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
SELECT  * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION
WHERE LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 1 

----------------------------NTILE() WITH PARTITION BY _ LOCATION _ LOWEST BUCKET  -----------------------------------------
WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
SELECT  * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION
WHERE LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 4 

----------------------------NTILE() WITH PARTITION BY + CASE + CTE QUERY  -----------------------------------------
WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		SALARY,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
),
NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL AS 

(
	SELECT  
		LOCATION,
		NEW_NAME,
		LOACTION_WISE_DATA,
		SALARY,
		CASE 
			WHEN LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 1 THEN 'FIRST_BUCKET'
			WHEN LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 2 THEN 'SECOND_BUCKET'
			WHEN LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 3 THEN 'THIRD_BUCKET'
			WHEN LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 4 THEN 'FOURTH_BUCKET'
		END AS BUCKET_WINDOWS
		FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION 
),
NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL_LOCATION AS 

(
	SELECT * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL
	WHERE BUCKET_WINDOWS = 'FIRST_BUCKET'
)
SELECT * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL_LOCATION
WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI'
ORDER BY SALARY DESC ;

--------------------------------------NTILE() + GROUP BY---------------------------------------------------------
--------------------------------------DATE : 29-08-2026----------------------------------------------------------

WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		LOCATION,
		SALARY,
		UPPER(CONCAT(FIRSTNAME, LASTNAME)) AS NEW_NAME,
		NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY EXPERIENCEYEARS DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA -------- 2 GROUPS MEANS2 BUCKET (TOTAL ROWS 100 SO :50 + 50 )------------- FROM 51 TO ANOTHER BUCKET LEVEL 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
)
		SELECT
			LOACTION_WISE_DATA,
			LOCATION,
			COUNT(*) TOTAL_EMPLOYEES,
			SUM(SALARY) AS SUM_SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY
			FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY LOACTION_WISE_DATA,LOCATION;

---------------------------------------------Level 7 — NTILE() + Aggregation----------------------------------

WITH NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		EMPLOYEEID,
		SUM(SALARY) AS NEW_SALARY                         
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION 
		GROUP BY EMPLOYEEID 
),
NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT
			EMPLOYEEID,
			NTILE(4) OVER (ORDER BY NEW_SALARY  DESC) AS LOACTION_WISE_DATA
			FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION
) 

SELECT * FROM NTILE_WINDOW_FUNCTION_FINAL	
WHERE LOACTION_WISE_DATA = 1 ;


-------------------------------------------------AGGRICATE WINFDOWS FUNCTIONS ---------------------------------------------------------------
WITH AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS AS 

(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			SUM(SALARY) AS SUM_VALAUE
			FROM  WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME

),
AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			SUM_VALAUE,
			SUM(SUM_VALAUE) OVER() AS DEPT_SALARY  -------------THIS IS WIOUT PARITITON BY DATA 
			FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS

)
SELECT * FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL;
-----------------------------------------MIN AGGRICATE FUNCTION WITH CTE -----------------------------------
WITH AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS AS 

(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			MIN(SALARY) AS MIN_SALARY_DATA
			FROM  WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME

),
AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			MIN_SALARY_DATA,
			MIN(MIN_SALARY_DATA) OVER() AS MIN_SALARY  -------------THIS IS WIOUT PARITITON BY DATA 
			FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS

)
SELECT * FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL;
-----------------------------------------MAX AGGRICATE FUNCTION WITH CTE -----------------------------------
WITH AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS AS 

(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			MAX(SALARY) AS MAX_SALARY_DATA
			FROM  WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME

),
AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			MAX_SALARY_DATA,
			MAX(MAX_SALARY_DATA) OVER() AS MIN_SALARY  -------------THIS IS WIOUT PARITITON BY DATA 
			FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS

)
SELECT * FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL;

-----------------------------------------AVG AGGRICATE FUNCTION WITH CTE -----------------------------------
WITH AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS AS 

(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY_DATA
			FROM  WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME

),
AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			AVG_SALARY_DATA,
			AVG(AVG_SALARY_DATA) OVER() AS MIN_SALARY -------------THIS IS WIOUT PARITITON BY DATA 
			FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS

)
SELECT * FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL ; 
-----------------------------------------COUNT AGGRICATE FUNCTION WITH CTE -----------------------------------
WITH AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS AS 

(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEE,
			AVG(SALARY) AS AVG_SALARY_DATA 
			FROM  WINDOWS_FUNCTION
			GROUP BY DEPARTMENTNAME

),
AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL AS 
(
		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			TOTAL_EMPLOYEE
			AVG_SALARY_DATA,
			AVG(AVG_SALARY_DATA) OVER() AS MIN_SALARY -------------THIS IS WIOUT PARITITON BY DATA 
			FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS

)
SELECT * FROM AGG_FUNCTION_CTE_WINDOWS_FINAL;

-------------------------------------------------------- PARTITION BY DEPARTMENT NAME -----------------------------------------

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			LOCATION ,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME) AS TOAL_SUM_SALARY ,
			AVG(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME) AS TOAL_SAVG_SALARY ,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME) AS TOAL_MIN_SALARY ,
			MAX(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME) AS TOAL_MAX_SALARY 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
-------------------------------------------------------- PARTITION BY LOCATION WISE  -----------------------------------------		

		SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			LOCATION ,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_SUM_SALARY ,
			AVG(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_SAVG_SALARY ,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_MIN_SALARY ,
			MAX(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_MAX_SALARY 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION


---------------------------ALL IN ONE QUERY --------------------------------------------------------
			
			
			SELECT 
			DEPARTMENTNAME,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			LOCATION ,
			ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS ROW_DATA, --------------------UNIQUE NUMBER DUPICATE RECORDS ALSO 
			RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS RANK_DATA, ------------------------- SAME NUMBER ASSIGN DUPICATES RECORDS + WITHOUTGAP 
			DENSE_RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS DENSE_RANK_DATA,-------------- SAME NUMBER ASSIGN DUPICATES RECORDS + WITH GAP 
			NTILE(4) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS BUCKET_GROUP,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_SUM_SALARY ,
			AVG(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_SAVG_SALARY ,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_MIN_SALARY ,
			MAX(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_MAX_SALARY ,
			COUNT(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY LOCATION) AS TOAL_MAX_SALARY 
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION


--------------------------------------------------DATE 30/08/2026---------------------------------------------------------------------

-----------------------To look at a value from a previous row without using a self-join.-----------------------------
------- THIS IS NORMAL SYNTAX OF LAG() FUNCTION ------------------------------
		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) ,
		LAG(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
-----------------THIS IS LAG() with Offset FUNCTION ---------------------******************------------------------

		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LAG(SALARY , 2) OVER ( ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WITH OFFSET FUNCTION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

----------------------------LAG() with Default Value--------------------*******************-------------------------
		 
		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LAG(SALARY , 2 , 0) OVER ( ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

------------------------------------LAG() with PARTITION BY------------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LAG(SALARY , 2 , 0) OVER ( PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
-------------------------------------Previous Salary Difference ⭐----------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		SALARY,
		SALARY - 
		LAG(SALARY ) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

-------------------Previous Salary Percentage Change THIS IS LITTLE BIT OF COMPLEX QUERY ----------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		SALARY,
		LAG(SALARY ) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID DESC) AS LAG_SALARY,
		(
			(	
				SALARY - 
				LAG(SALARY ) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID DESC)  --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
			) * 100 /
				LAG(SALARY ) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID DESC) 
				
		) AS SALARY_CHANGED_PERCENTAGE
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
		ORDER BY SALARY DESC 
-------------------LAG() with Dates ⭐-----------------------------------------------

		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		HIREDATE,
		BONUS,
		LAG(BONUS) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY HIREDATE ) AS LAG_DATE
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		HIREDATE,
		LAG(HIREDATE) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY HIREDATE) AS LAG_DATE
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

		---------------Detect Changes ⭐⭐⭐------------------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		EMPLOYMENTSTATUS,
		LAG(EMPLOYMENTSTATUS) OVER ( PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY HIREDATE) AS DETECT_CHANGES
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

		-----------------LAG() + CASE--------------------------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		CASE
			WHEN SALARY > LAG(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'INCRESED'
			WHEN SALARY < LAG(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'DECRESED'
			ELSE'NOCHANGE'
		END AS SALARY_STATUS
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

		-----------------LAG() + CTE--------------------------------------------------
WITH LAG_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME AS NEW_NAME,
		SALARY AS NEW_SALARY,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS MERGED_NAME,
		CASE
			WHEN SALARY > LAG(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'INCRESED'
			WHEN SALARY < LAG(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'DECRESED'
			ELSE'NOCHANGE'
		END AS SALARY_STATUS,
		LAG(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
),
LAG_FUNCTION_FINAL AS 
(
	SELECT
		NEW_NAME,
		MERGED_NAME,
		NEW_SALARY,
		SALARY_STATUS,
		NEW_SALARY - LAG_SALARY AS SALARY_DIFFERENCE
		FROM LAG_FUNCTION
)
	SELECT 
		MERGED_NAME,
		CASE 
			WHEN SALARY_STATUS = 'INCRESED' THEN '2'
			WHEN SALARY_STATUS = 'DECRESED' THEN '1'
			ELSE '0'
		END AS FINAL_DATA
		FROM LAG_FUNCTION_FINAL
	

-----------------------To look at a value from a previous row without using a self-join.-----------------
------- THIS IS NORMAL SYNTAX OF LEAD() FUNCTION ------------------------------
		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) ,
		LEAD(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
-----------------THIS IS LEAD() with Offset FUNCTION ---------------------******************-------------

		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LEAD(SALARY , 2) OVER ( ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WITH OFFSET FUNCTION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

----------------------------LEAD() with Default Value--------------------*******************---------
		 
		SELECT 
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LAG(SALARY , 2 , 0) OVER ( ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

------------------------------------LEAD() with PARTITION BY------------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		LEAD(SALARY , 2 , 0) OVER ( PARTITION BY DEPARTMENTNAME ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LAG_SALARY_OFFSET --------------- WE WILL PUT DEFAULT VALUE IN NULL LOCATION 
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION

			-----------------LAG() + CASE--------------------------------------------------
		SELECT 
		DEPARTMENTNAME,
		CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME),
		CASE
			WHEN SALARY > LEAD(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'INCRESED'
			WHEN SALARY < LEAD(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) THEN 'DECRESED'
			ELSE'NOCHANGE'
		END AS SALARY_STATUS
		FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION;

------------------------------------------LEAD() + CTE + CASE----------------------------------
WITH LEAD_FUNCTION AS 
(
		SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS MERGED_NAME,
			GENDER AS NEW_GENDER,
			AGE AS NEW_AGE,
			SALARY,
			DEPARTMENTNAME AS NEW_DEPARTMENT,
			LOCATION AS NEW_LOCATION,
			LEAD(SALARY , 3) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY DESC) AS LEAD_SALARY
			FROM WINDOWS_FUNCTION
),
LEAD_FUNCTION_FINAL AS 
(
SELECT 
	MERGED_NAME,
	NEW_GENDER,
	NEW_AGE,
	NEW_DEPARTMENT,
	NEW_LOCATION,
	CASE
		WHEN LEAD_SALARY IS NULL THEN  'LAST_RECORD'
		WHEN LEAD_SALARY > SALARY THEN 'NEXT_SALARY_HIGHER'
		WHEN LEAD_SALARY < SALARY THEN 'NEXT_SALARY_LOWER'
		ELSE'NO_CHANGE'
	END AS NEW_LEAD_FUNCTION
	FROM LEAD_FUNCTION
)
SELECT * FROM LEAD_FUNCTION_FINAL
WHERE NEW_LEAD_FUNCTION = 'LAST_RECORD'


--------------------=======================================------------------------------------===============================
		/*FIRST_VALUE()
		→ Returns the first value from the defined window/order.

		LAST_VALUE()
		→ Returns the last value from the defined window/order.
		*/
--------------------=======================================------------------------------------===============================

	------------------ THIS IS NORMAL FIRS VALUE ORDER BU SALARY DESC ----------------------------------------	
		SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('__' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_MERGED_NAME,
			IIF (GENDER = 'M', 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') NEW_GENDER,
			TRIM(LOCATION) AS SPACED_REMOVED,
			UPPER(JOBTITLE) AS UPPER_JOB,
			SALARY,
			BONUS,
			CAST(HIREDATE AS DATE) AS NEW_HIRE_DATE,
			STATUS,
			FIRST_VALUE(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY) AS FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION  ------------ THIS IS ONLY ORDER BY QUERY 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS

-------------------- THIS IS USING WITH PARITION BY OORDER BY SALAR DESC --------------------------------

	SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('__' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_MERGED_NAME,
			IIF (GENDER = 'M', 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') NEW_GENDER,
			TRIM(LOCATION) AS SPACED_REMOVED,
			UPPER(JOBTITLE) AS UPPER_JOB,
			SALARY,
			BONUS,
			CAST(HIREDATE AS DATE) AS NEW_HIRE_DATE,
			STATUS,
			FIRST_VALUE(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY JOBTITLE ORDER BY SALARY) AS FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION  --------------- THIS WITH PARTITON QUERY 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;
------------------------------------ WITH CASE AND FIRST() VALUE FUNCTION ---------------------------------
	
WITH FIRST_VALUE_FUNCION AS
(
	SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('__' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_MERGED_NAME,
			IIF (GENDER = 'M', 'MALE' , 'FEMALE') NEW_GENDER,
			TRIM(LOCATION) AS SPACED_REMOVED,
			UPPER(JOBTITLE) AS UPPER_JOB,
			SALARY AS NEW_SALARY,
			BONUS AS NEW_BONUS,
			CAST(HIREDATE AS DATE) AS NEW_HIRE_DATE,
			STATUS AS NEW_SATUS,
			FIRST_VALUE (SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY JOBTITLE ORDER BY SALARY) AS FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION , --------------- THIS WITH PARTITON QUERY 
			SALARY - FIRST_VALUE (SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY JOBTITLE ORDER BY SALARY) AS SALARY_MINUS_DATA 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
),

FINAL_FIRST_FUNCTION AS 
(
	SELECT 
			NEW_MERGED_NAME,
			NEW_GENDER,
			SPACED_REMOVED,
			UPPER_JOB,
			NEW_SALARY,
			NEW_HIRE_DATE,
			FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION,
			SALARY_MINUS_DATA,
			CASE 
				WHEN SALARY_MINUS_DATA  = 0 THEN 'FIRST_VALUE'
				WHEN SALARY_MINUS_DATA > FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION THEN 'HIGH'
				WHEN SALARY_MINUS_DATA < FIRST_VALAUE_FUNCTION THEN 'LOW'
				ELSE 'NO_CHANGE'
			END AS FINAL_DATA
			FROM FIRST_VALUE_FUNCION
)

SELECT * FROM FINAL_FIRST_FUNCTION
WHERE FINAL_DATA = 'FIRST_VALUE'


--------	LAST_VALUE()----------------------------------
--------	→ Returns the last value from the defined window/order.-----------------
-----	*/-----------------------------------
------------------ THIS IS NORMAL FIRS VALUE ORDER BU SALARY DESC ----------------------------------------	

------- THE MAIN CONCEPT IN LAST() IS ----------------
/*
ROWS BETWEEN <START> AND <END> -----------THE GENERAL STRUCTURE OF THE LAST_VALUE QUERY-------
ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW ---------------- CURRENT ROW ONLY --------------
ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ------------- FROM BEGINNING  TO CURRENT ROW ----------------
ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING ------------ BEGINNING CURRENT ROW END-----------------
ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING ---------- ENTIRE WINDOW ROW-------------------
ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ------------------------ Previous 1 row → current row ---------
ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ----------------------- Previous 2 rows → current row----------------
ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 1 FOLLOWING ----------------------Current row → next 1 row-------------------
ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING --------------------------Current row → next 2 rows-----------------
ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING AND 2 FOLLOWING --------------------Previous 2 rows → next 2 rows --------------
*/

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------------------------BASIC — Entire Window-------------------------------------------------------
		SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			SALARY,
				LAST_VALUE(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY SALARY 
				ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING) AS LAST_VALUE_FUNCTION  ------------ THIS IS THE LAS_VALUE_FUNCTION () ---Every row can see the entire window.
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS

-------------------- THIS IS USING WITH PARITION BY OORDER BY SALAR DESC --------------------------------

		SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			SALARY,
			JOBTITLE,
				LAST_VALUE(SALARY) OVER ( PARTITION BY JOBTITLE  ORDER BY JOBTITLE 
				ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING) AS LAST_VALUE_FUNCTION  ------------ THIS IS THE LAS_VALUE_FUNCTION () --Every row can see the entire window.
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
---------------------------------------------------------BASIC — Beginning → Current Row-------------------------------------------------------
--------------This is the classic running total.------------------
		SELECT 
			CONCAT_WS('_' , FIRSTNAME , LASTNAME) AS NEW_NAME,
			SALARY,
			JOBTITLE,
				LAST_VALUE(SALARY) OVER ( ORDER BY  EMPLOYEEID
				ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) AS LAST_VALUE_FUNCTION  ------------ This is the classic running total.
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

--------------------------------------------------------     DATE : 31/08/2026     --------------------------------------------------------

		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW MEANS WILL CHECK EACH BY ROW 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

		
		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- FROM THE FIRST ROW TO UNTIL LAST ROW  
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME -------------  Previous 1 row → current row
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  2 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME -------------  Previous 2 row → current row
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  CURRENT ROW AND 1 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME -------------  CURRENT ROW + NEXT ROW 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  CURRENT ROW AND 2 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME -------------  CURRENT ROW + NEXT ROW 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

		
		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  1 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- Previous row + current + next row
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

		
		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- From first row to current row
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'


		----SUM FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
		SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			SUM(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN  CURRENT ROW AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- From current row to last row
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING  AND 1 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW  AND 2 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS
			WHERE LOCATION = 'CHENNAI' AND GENDER = 'M'

			/*
			UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING
			→ Entire window: first row → last row

			UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
			→ Starting row → current row

			CURRENT ROW AND UNBOUNDED FOLLOWING
			→ Current row → last row

			CURRENT ROW AND 1 FOLLOWING
			→ Current row + next row

			1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW
			→ Previous row + current row

			1 PRECEDING AND 1 FOLLOWING
			→ Previous row + current row + next row

			CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW
			→ Current row only
			*/
			
			----MAX FUNCTION WINDOW FRAME -----------------
			SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			SALARY,
			MAX(SALARY) OVER (ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME ------------- CURRENT ROW AND CURRENT ROW MEANS WILL CHECK EACH BY ROW 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

---------------------Next level of Moving Calculation-------------------------------
------------------------------Now practice business-oriented rolling calculations, such as:--------------------------
---------3-row moving average salary ----------------------------

	SELECT 
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			GENDER,
			LOCATION,
			JOBTITLE,
			SALARY,
			MIN(SALARY) OVER (PARTITION BY JOBTITLE ORDER BY EMPLOYEEID ROWS BETWEEN 2 PRECEDING  AND 2 FOLLOWING ) AS CURNT_ROW_FRAME 
			FROM EMPLOYEES_WINDOWS;

---------------------------------ADVANCED DATA ENGINEERING CONVEPTS -------------------------
---------------------------------DEDUPLICATIONS ---------------------------------------------

SELECT * FROM DATA_CLEANING 

--STEP 2 : -----------------------------TO FIND THE DUPLICATION RECORDS USING GROUP BY FUNCTION -------------------------------

SELECT 
	COUNT(*)AS TOAL_EMPLOYEES,
	CUSTOMERID
	FROM DATA_CLEANING
	GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
	HAVING COUNT(*)= 1 

----STEP 3 : --------------------CREATE ROW NUMBER FOR DEDUPLICATION--------------------------------------

WITH DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL AS 
(
	SELECT 
		*,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS DEDUPLICATION_REOCRDS
		FROM DATA_CLEANING
)
	SELECT * 
	FROM DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL;


---STEP 4 : --------------- ---------KEEP ONLY LATEST RECORD------------------------------------------

WITH DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL AS 
(
	SELECT 
		*,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS DEDUPLICATION_REOCRDS
		FROM DATA_CLEANING
)
	SELECT * 
	FROM DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL
	WHERE  DEDUPLICATION_REOCRDS = 1 
	ORDER BY EmployeeID

----STEP 5 : ----------------------------------LOAD DEDUPLICATED DATA INTO TARGET---------------------- THIS IS OPTION 2 TO LOAD THE DATA TO TARGET TABLE --------

WITH DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL AS 
(
	SELECT 
		*,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS DEDUPLICATION_REOCRDS
		FROM DATA_CLEANING
)
SELECT 
	EMPLOYEEID,
	CUSTOMERID,
	CUSTOMERNAME,
	LOCATION,
	DEPARTMENT,
	SALARY,
	STATUS,
	HIREDATE,
	UPDATEDDATETIME,
	SOURCESYSTEM
INTO DATA_CLEANING_TARGET
FROM DUPLICATION_REOCRDS_FINAL
WHERE DEDUPLICATION_REOCRDS =1 

---- STEP 6 :-----------------------------VALIDATE TARGET-------------------------------


SELECT 
	COUNT(*)AS TOTALEMPLOYEES,
	CUSTOMERID
	FROM DATA_CLEANING_TARGET
	GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
	HAVING COUNT(*)> 1 ----------------------- AFTER  THIS QUERY THERE IS NO DUPLICATES RECORD IN TARGET TABLE --------------------------

----- STEP 7: --------------------CHECK THE NULL VALUES IN BUSINESS KY ---------------------------

	SELECT 
		* FROM DATA_CLEANING_TARGET
		WHERE CUSTOMERID IS NULL  -------------- THIS ALSO OUPUT IS EMPTY BECAUSE THE TARGET TABLE IS CORRECT

----- STEP 8 : -----------------------------CHECK SOURCE VS TARGET COUNT-----------------------------

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DATA_CLEANING
	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM DATA_CLEANING_TARGET

	
----- STEP - 1 : SOURCE DATA --- ALREADY EXISTS 

	SELECT * FROM MNC_DATA --------- SEE WAHT DATA HAS ARRIVED FROM THE SOURCE TABLE
	
------ STEP - 2 :  LOAD SOURCE INTO STAGING 
	SELECT * INTO MNC_DATA_STAGING -------- IN A REAL COMPANY, THE INGESTION TOOLS MAY LOAD THE STAGING TABLE INSTED OF SQL SELECT INTO
	FROM MNC_DATA 

------ STEP - 3 : FRIST UNDERSTAND HOW MANY RECORDS AVAILBALE 
	
	SELECT COUNT (*)  AS TOTAL_DATA -------- UNDERSTAND THE INCOMING DATA BEFORE DEDUPLICATION 
	FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING 

---- STEP -  4 : IDENTIY THE BUSINESS KEY -------

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_CUSTOMERID
		FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID  ----------- UNDERSTAND HOW MANY RECORDS EXISTS FOR EACH CUTOMER
		ORDER BY TOTAL_CUSTOMERID DESC 

---- STEP  - 5 : FIND DUPLICATE BUSINESS KEY 

SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_CUSTOMERID
		FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID  
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1  ----- FIND THE CUSTOMERS HAVING 
		ORDER BY TOTAL_CUSTOMERID DESC 

---- STEP  - 6 CREATE ROW_NUMBER FUNCTION 

	WITH ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION AS 
	(
		SELECT * ,
			ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS DEDUPLICATE_RECORDS
			FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
	)
		SELECT * FROM ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION ; --- LASTEST RECORD = 1 , OLD RECORDS 2 , 3, 4 ,5 ,6 ,7.....


----- STEP  - 7 WIHTOUT DUPLICATES RECORDS 

	WITH ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION AS 
	(
		SELECT * ,
			ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS DEDUPLICATE_RECORDS
			FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
	)
		SELECT * FROM ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION  --- LASTEST RECORD = 1 , OLD RECORDS 2 , 3, 4 ,5 ,6 ,7.....
		WHERE DEDUPLICATE_RECORDS = 1 

------- STEP - 8 LOAD DUPLICATED RECORDS TO TARGET TABLE 

WITH ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION AS 
	(
		SELECT * ,
			ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS DEDUPLICATE_RECORDS
			FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
	)
		SELECT 
			SOURCERECORDID,
			CUSTOMERID,
			FIRSTNAME,
			LASTNAME,
			CITY,
			COUNTRY,
			DEPARTMENT,
			ANNUALSALARY,
			STATUS,
			HIREDATE,
			UPDATEDDATETIME,
			SOURCESYSTEM,
			BATCHID
			INTO MNC_DATA_TARGET
			FROM ROW_NUMBER_DUPLICATION
			WHERE DEDUPLICATE_RECORDS = 1 
			AND CUSTOMERID IS NOT NULL 

-------- STEP - 9 - VALIDATE THE TARGET TABLE DUPLICATED 
	
		SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES
		FROM MNC_DATA_TARGET
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 

----- STEP  - 10 VALIDATE TARGET COUNT AND SOURCE COUNT 

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM MNC_DATA_STAGING
	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM MNC_DATA_TARGET



------------------------- DATA DEDUPLICATION PRACTICE ---------------------------------------------------


CREATE DATABASE DEDUPLICATION 


--- STEP 1 :  THIS IS THE SOURCE FILE STEP : 1 ----- GET IT FROM DATABASE OR FILES OR CLOUD OR API 

	SELECT  * FROM CRM_SOURCE ;

----STEP : 2  THIS IS FOR TO CHANGE THE SOURCE FILE TO STAGGING 

	SELECT * INTO CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
	FROM CRM_SOURCE ;

--- STEP : 3  TO CHECK HOW MANY RECORDS AVIALABLE IN STAGGING TABLE 
	SELECT * FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING; ------ FIRST UNDERSTAND THE HOW MANY RECORDS AVAILABLE IN TABLE 

----STEP : 4  NOW WE HAVE TO FIND THE DUPLICATES RECORDS 
	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES ------------- UNDERS STAND THE HOW MANY RECORDS AVAILABLE IN EACH CUSTOMERID 
		FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID 

--- STEP : 5 FILTER THE DUPLICATED RECORDS 

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_EMPLOYEES
		FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
		ORDER BY TOTAL_EMPLOYEES DESC 

---- STEP : 6 CREATE ROW_NUMBER() FUNCTION ---------------

WITH STAGING_FILES AS 
(
	SELECT
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC ) AS FIND_DUPLICATED_RECORDS
		FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
)
SELECT * FROM STAGING_FILES ;

--STEP : 7 FIND ORIGINAL RECORDS USING WINDOWS FUNCTION 

WITH STAGING_FILES AS 
(
	SELECT
		CUSTOMERID,
		STATUS,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC ) AS FIND_DUPLICATED_RECORDS
		FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
)
SELECT * FROM STAGING_FILES
WHERE FIND_DUPLICATED_RECORDS = 1 
AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE' 

--- STEP 8 : LOAD INTO TARGET TABLE 


WITH STAGING_FILES AS 
(
	SELECT
		* ,
		ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC ) AS FIND_DUPLICATED_RECORDS
		FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
)
	SELECT
		CUSTOMERID,
		EMPLOYEEID,
		CUSTOMERNAME,
		LOCATION,
		DEPARTMENT,
		SALARY,
		STATUS,
		HIREDATE,
		UPDATEDDATETIME,
		SOURCESYSTEM
		INTO CRM_COURCE_FINAL
		FROM STAGING_FILES
		WHERE FIND_DUPLICATED_RECORDS = 1 
		AND STATUS = 'ACTIVE'
		AND CUSTOMERID IS NOT NULL 

--- STEP : 9 VALIDATE THE TARGET TABLE DUPLICATED 

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_DATA
		FROM CRM_COURCE_FINAL
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 
		
--- STEP : 10  TO CHECK STAGING TABLE AND TARGET TABLE HOW MANY RECORDS AVAILABLE 

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM CRM_SOURCE_STAGING
	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM CRM_COURCE_FINAL 



------------------------------- FILE 2 CHECKING ------------------------------------------------------------


-- STEP : 1 

	SELECT * FROM ERP_SOURCE 

------------LOAD THE SOURCE FILE TO STAGING FILE ------------------------------------------	

	SELECT * INTO ERP_SOURCE_STAGING FROM ERP_SOURCE 

	SELECT * FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING

---------- CHECKING THE DUPLICATED RECORDS USING THE CUSTOMERID ----------------------------------

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_DATA
		FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID

----------------- TO CHECK THE MORE THAN 1 RECORDS IN THAT TABLE --------------------

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_DATA
		FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 

------------- ROW_NUMBER() USED FOR FIND THE DUPLICATES RECORDS ALSO----------------

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS FINAL_RECORDS
		 FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING

----------------- USE CTE LOGIC FOR TO FIND THE RECORDS USING ROW_NUMBER --------------------------

WITH FINAl_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS FINAL_RECORDS
		 FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
)
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA

---------------TO FIND THE LATEST RECORDS IN TABLE -----------------------------

WITH FINAl_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS FINAL_RECORDS
		 FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
)
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA
WHERE FINAL_RECORDS = 1 

---------- TO LOAD THE DATA INTO TARGET FILE --------------------

WITH FINAl_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		*,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER (PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME) AS FINAL_RECORDS
		 FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
)
SELECT 
	*
	INTO ERP_SOURCE_FINAL
	FROM FINAL_DATA
	WHERE FINAL_RECORDS = 1 

-- FINAL TO CHECK THE BOTH FILE COUNTING DATA 

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ERP_SOURCE_STAGING
	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ERP_SOURCE_FINAL

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID

------ TO FIND MORE THAN ONE RECORSD IN STAGING FILE ---------------------------------


	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		COUNT(*) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING
		GROUP BY CUSTOMERID
		HAVING COUNT(*) > 1 

----- TO USE ROW() FUNCTION FOR TO FIND TH DUPKLCIATED RECORDS

	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER( PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING

---------- TO CHECK THE DUPLCIATE RECORDS WITH CTE --------------

WITH FINAL_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER( PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING
) 
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 

------- FIND THE DUPLICATED RECORDS-----------


WITH FINAL_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		CUSTOMERID,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER( PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING
) 
SELECT * FROM FINAL_DATA 
WHERE TOTAL_RECORDS = 1 


----- LOADED THE DATA INTO FINALE TABLE ---------------------


WITH FINAL_DATA AS 
(
	SELECT 
		*,
		ROW_NUMBER () OVER( PARTITION BY CUSTOMERID ORDER BY UPDATEDDATETIME DESC) AS TOTAL_RECORDS
		FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_STAGING
) 
SELECT * 
INTO GLOBAL_SOURCE_FINAL
FROM FINAL_DATA 
WHERE TOTAL_RECORDS = 1

	SELECT COUNT(*) FROM GLOBAL_SOURCE_FINAL
